## 2026-11-07T02:17:34Z - P02.3a
- I added the `TextRuntime` port and the `KoboldCppRuntime` adapter, and tested the adapter against `fake_kobold`. Lint passes, and the targeted unit run passed: all 19 new adapter tests plus the related backend tests.

What changed:
- **The port** (`backend/src/lyrveil/ports/text_runtime.py`): the `TextRuntime` protocol, the request, chunk and info types, and the typed errors. Each error has a `code` that matches docs/api.md:
  - `RuntimeUnavailableError` (`RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE`), with two subclasses: `RuntimeStreamDroppedError` and `RuntimeResponseInvalidError`.
  - `RuntimeOutOfMemoryError` (`LLM_OOM`).
  - `GenerationStalledError` (`GENERATION_STALLED`).
  - `GenerationTimeoutError` (`GENERATION_TIMEOUT`).
- **The adapter** (`backend/src/lyrveil/runtimes/koboldcpp.py`) calls only seven documented endpoints, listed in its `ENDPOINTS`. It does not call `perf`.
  - **Streaming:** tokens come in order, then a final chunk with the finish reason and the whole text.
  - **Abort:** `abort(genkey)` ends a running stream, and its final chunk then reports "aborted".
  - **Token counts:** these come only from `POST /api/extra/tokencount`.
  - **Timeouts:** 180 s in total and 60 s without a stream line, from docs/gpu-orchestration.md. Past either one, the adapter aborts the generation before it raises. Other calls wait 10 s; that value is my own choice because the timeout table has none.
- **The tests** (`tests/unit/test_koboldcpp_runtime.py`) cover:
  - streaming, abort, a dropped stream, a stall and the total timeout;
  - token counts, generate and info;
  - out-of-memory, invalid JSON and crash replies on all three POST endpoints;
  - a check that reads docs/runtime-contracts.md and fails if the adapter's module names an `/api/` path missing from the KoboldCpp endpoint table (with a test showing the check catches one), or if a request sends a field outside the GenerationInput table.
- **Dependencies and build:** HTTPX 0.28.1 moved from the backend's dev group to its runtime dependencies. I edited `uv.lock` by hand to match, because uv is not available on Windows. The lint build ran `uv sync --locked` on it without error. The backend test stage now copies `tests/fakes` and the contract page, and pytest, mypy and ruff now include ".".
- **Docs:** docs/backend.md has a new Text runtime section and updated port signatures; docs/testing.md now says the backend test stage copies the fakes.

Assumptions and decisions:
- Treating an error answer that says "out of memory", or a closing stream event with `finish_reason` "error", as out of memory is an assumption shared with the fake. Any closing "error" counts as out of memory.
- The field name `value` for the context size comes from the fake, not from the contract.
- I changed `abort` to return a bool instead of None, and renamed the errors with an `Error` suffix to match the lint rules.

Not run: the integration and end-to-end levels, and anything against a real KoboldCpp. I left CHANGELOG.md alone because nothing calls the adapter yet, so users would not notice the change.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/ports/__init__.py, backend/src/lyrveil/ports/text_runtime.py, backend/src/lyrveil/runtimes/__init__.py, backend/src/lyrveil/runtimes/koboldcpp.py, backend/tests/unit/test_koboldcpp_runtime.py, backend/tests/unit/test_locked_environment.py, backend/pyproject.toml, backend/uv.lock, backend/Dockerfile, docs/backend.md, docs/testing.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - KoboldCppRuntime is async (HTTPX AsyncClient with trust_env=False). Use it as `async with KoboldCppRuntime(url)`. Its default URL is http://lyrveil-llm:5001. Nothing calls it yet; the P02.4 job queue should.
  - TextRuntime.abort returns bool. A stream whose genkey was aborted ends with finish_reason 'aborted'. Every TextRuntimeError subclass has a class-level `code` that matches the docs/api.md job error codes.
  - The backend test stage now holds /app/fakes and /app/docs/runtime-contracts.md. Backend tests can `from fakes.fake_kobold import FakeKobold`. Async backend tests use `@pytest.mark.anyio` with an `anyio_backend` fixture returning 'asyncio'.
  - A small uv.lock change, such as moving a package already in the lock from dev to runtime, can be hand-edited in the root package's dependencies, dev-dependencies, requires-dist and requires-dev. `test lint` builds the test stage with `uv sync --locked` and so checks the edit.
  - Ruff PT012 is active: a `pytest.raises` block must hold one simple statement, so move loops and branches into helper functions.
  - Two assumptions are still unconfirmed until the GPU suite: the field name `value` for /api/extra/true_max_context_length, and the out-of-memory detection (body text 'out of memory', or a closing stream event with 'error').

## 2026-11-07T02:23:11Z - P02.3b
- I added `PromptFormatter` for `gemma4`, `mistral_v3_tekken` and `chatml` in `backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/formatter.py`, following docs/prompting.md (Prompt format) and ADRs 0025 and 0031.

What it does:
- `formatter_for(family)` returns the formatter for a catalog template.
- `chat(parts, current_message)` lays out a reply prompt: the stable text, the history as plain turns, then the last user turn. That turn holds the frame of the dynamic context and the current message.
- `proactive(parts, instruction)` uses the same layout, but the frame ends with the instruction and has no `[Messaggio di …]` line.
- `structured(system, user)` gives one system text and one user turn, with no frame.
- `stop_sequences(user_display_name)` returns each family's stop list.
- `chat` and `proactive` return `FormattedPrompt(text, prefix)`. `prefix` is the part of `text` before the last user turn: the stable text plus history for `gemma4` and `chatml`, only the history for `mistral_v3_tekken`.
- `neutralize_frame_markers` turns the `[` into `(` on any line starting with `[Contesto per` or `[Messaggio di` (any case, optional leading spaces, also after a lone `\r`). It is applied to every text the formatter receives, so only the formatter itself writes real markers. No family writes BOS.

Tests: `backend/tests/unit/test_prompt_formatter.py` holds 24 cases for T-MOD-02 and T-MOD-05. The golden strings are written from the formats in docs/prompting.md, because sessions have no access to the official templates; T-GPU-03 will confirm them. The tests check:
- the golden strings for chat and structured prompts;
- the stop sequences;
- that no family writes BOS;
- that two consecutive replies share the prefix byte for byte, and old turns carry no frame;
- that markers are neutralized in the summary, user and character turns, memories, the current message and the proactive instruction;
- the proactive layout, empty blocks being dropped, and that neutralizing twice changes nothing.

Checks: `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/unit/test_prompt_formatter.py` passed (24 tests). `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` failed once on `ruff format`; I fixed the layout and it then passed. The final format fix only moved line breaks and came after that test run. I did not run the integration, e2e or Docker levels, or anything against a real model.

Choices the docs did not fix, recorded in docs/backend.md: an empty block 4, 5 or 9 is left out of the frame, and trailing line breaks of a block are dropped. Each history turn is written under its own role, so two character turns in a row produce two `{reply}</s>` pieces in `mistral_v3_tekken`, an order its GGUF template would refuse.

Finding, outside this task: nothing removes control tokens such as `<|turn>`, `[INST]` or `<|im_start|>` that a user types into a message. KoboldCpp reads control tokens written in the prompt as real tokens, so this is a possible prompt injection that no document covers yet.

CHANGELOG.md is unchanged because nothing calls the formatter yet.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/__init__.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/formatter.py, backend/tests/unit/test_prompt_formatter.py, docs/backend.md, docs/prompting.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - PromptFormatter is in lyrveil.prompts.formatter. Use formatter_for(catalog template) and then .chat(ChatParts, msg), .proactive(ChatParts, instruction), .structured(system, user) and .stop_sequences(user_name). Nothing calls it yet; ContextBuilder should. Its stop_sequences fill TextRequest.stop_sequence.
  - FormattedPrompt.prefix ends where the last user turn begins. For mistral_v3_tekken the stable text sits in the last turn and is not part of the prefix.
  - The formatter neutralizes frame markers in every text it receives, so callers must not write '[Contesto per' or '[Messaggio di' themselves.
  - Gap, not covered by any doc: nothing removes control tokens (<|turn>, [INST], <|im_start|>) from user text, and KoboldCpp parses control tokens written in the prompt. This needs a product or security decision.
  - Running one test: `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/unit/<file>.py` runs only that file in the backend test stage.
  - ruff format joins wrapped expressions back onto one line when they fit in 120 characters. Run `test lint` after hand-wrapping lines.

## 2026-11-07T02:36:50Z - P02.4
- The durable job queue from ADR 0010 is in place. Revision 0002 creates `generation_jobs` with CHECK lists, json_valid checks, a partial unique index on idempotency_key and the selection index. The `jobs/` package has four modules:
- `policy.py` holds the kinds, priorities, classes, attempts, backoffs and the stage-to-mode mapping.
- `queue.py` holds `JobQueue`. It works on a connection the caller owns and never commits, so a job is committed or rolled back with the change that creates it.
- `handlers.py` defines the handler contract: `JobDone`, `NextStage`, `JobFailedError` and `JobPreemptedError`.
- `scheduler.py` holds `JobScheduler`. It runs one job at a time and only when its stage needs `none` or the mode the GPU is in. A refused or unanswered lease leaves the job queued. It renews the lease every 10 s; a lost lease cancels the handler and retries with LEASE_LOST. Each failure code maps to a lease release outcome (oom, runtime_error or ok).

Retries use the kind's backoff and maximum, then end `failed` with error_code and error_detail. `main()` now requeues interrupted `running` jobs at startup (step 5). The interrupted run counts as an attempt, and a job on its last attempt fails with JOB_INTERRUPTED. `domain/identifiers.py` adds UUIDv7 ids and UTC timestamp text with milliseconds.

Checks I observed:
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed, after fixing ruff findings and line breaks.
- `test unit tests/unit/test_job_policy.py`: 5 passed.
- `test unit` on tests/integration/test_job_queue.py, test_migrations.py and test_health_and_startup.py: 31 passed. The integration level takes no file arguments, so I ran these files through the unit level's pytest.
- `test unit` on test_cli.py, test_import_rules.py and test_logs.py: 41 passed.

I did not run the full unit, integration, e2e or docker levels. Nothing has run against a real orchestrator or KoboldCpp.

No handler is registered and the worker loop is not started yet; P02.5 does that. Mode requests, batching, cancellation and cross-mode preemption are left to P06.3.

Doc findings: the retry table gives extraction three waits but only 3 attempts, so the 30 min wait is never used. image-generation.md says `stage` records the last finished step, while the code uses it as the step to run next. Backoffs for character_draft, visual_profile_translate and proactive_message were not documented; I chose 5 s. All of these are recorded in docs/backend.md (Job queue). I found no injected instructions in the files I read.
- Files changed: backend/migrations/versions/0002_generation_jobs.py, backend/src/lyrveil/domain/__init__.py, backend/src/lyrveil/domain/identifiers.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/__init__.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/policy.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/queue.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/handlers.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/scheduler.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/tests/integration/test_job_queue.py, backend/tests/integration/test_migrations.py, backend/tests/integration/test_health_and_startup.py, backend/tests/unit/test_job_policy.py, docs/backend.md, docs/api.md, docs/data-model.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - To create a job, call JobQueue.enqueue(connection, kind, payload, idempotency_key=...) inside the same write(engine, fn) transaction as the change that creates it. lyrveil.jobs.scheduler.read and write wrap a read, or a BEGIN IMMEDIATE commit, around a callable.
  - The P02.5 chat handlers must be registered in JobScheduler(engine, queue, {JobKind.CHAT_REPLY: handler}, OrchestratorClient, current_mode). run_forever() still has to be started in the app lifespan, and current_mode needs a GPU state source; the long poll is not built yet.
  - Handlers return JobDone(result) or NextStage(stage, payload). They raise JobFailedError(code, detail, retryable=...) or JobPreemptedError, or let a TextRuntimeError through, which is retried with its code. Any other exception ends the job with INTERNAL. Handlers must be idempotent per stage.
  - A chat_reply that a restart interrupted is requeued automatically. If P02.5 wants 'interrupted variant plus offered regeneration' instead, the chat handler must decide that on its next run.
  - Alembic head is now 0002. Tests read head_revision() instead of hard-coding it.
  - `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test integration` takes no arguments. To run single integration files, use `test unit tests/integration/<file>.py`, which works because the conftest is picked up.
  - The log allowlist renders None identifier values as '[dropped]', so leave out optional fields such as error_code when they are None.

## 2026-11-07T02:49:42Z - P02.5b
- Added messages to the backend: send with an idempotency key, edit, delete, regenerate into variants with selectable alternatives, and history paging. This is migration 0004, a new domain, repository, service and API router, and docs. I did not run the full unit, integration, e2e or docker levels.

**What it does**
- **Send** saves the user message and a queued reply placeholder in one transaction, then queues the `chat_reply` job in a second one. A full queue therefore can't lose the user's text. Retrying with the same `client_message_id` returns the first result, and queues the job if the first attempt died before queuing it.
- **Regenerate** works only on the last visible message, and only when a character wrote it. It adds an active `generated` variant and queues `chat_regenerate`. The old variants stay and can be switched back to with activate.
- **Edit** adds an `edited` variant, and editing to the same text changes nothing. **Delete** is a soft delete.
- **History** returns pages of the newest messages below `before_seq`, oldest first inside each page. Queued, failed and interrupted replies are included with their status.

**Choices I made without a spec**
- Send and regenerate in an archived conversation answer 409.
- The `image` intent answers 409 until P05.
- If the queue is full, the new reply or variant is marked failed and the call answers 409. A retry with the same `client_message_id` queues it again.

**Still open**
- The new routes are not in `backend/openapi.json` yet, same as the conversation routes.
- Nothing answers the queued replies until P02.5c adds the handlers and starts the worker.
- I did not add the SET NULL keys from `generation_jobs` to `conversations`, `characters` and `messages` (batch rebuild of the job table). I couldn't verify that rebuild here, and the docs say so.
- Edit and delete start no memory re-evaluation yet (P04.7).
- Files changed: backend/migrations/versions/0004_messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/domain/messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/domain/errors.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/api/messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/api/security.py, backend/src/lyrveil/api/conversations.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/tests/integration/test_messages.py, backend/tests/integration/test_migrations.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/api.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/backend.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Alembic head is now 0004. `messages` and `message_variants` exist. The job table's `conversation_id`, `character_id` and `message_id` are still plain columns, because I did not rebuild it with SET NULL keys.
  - A send creates a user message (complete) and a reply placeholder (queued, empty text) with consecutive `seq`, plus a `chat_reply` job. The job's `message_id` is the reply, its `idempotency_key` is `chat_reply:<user id>`, and its payload holds `user_message_id`, `reply_message_id`, `variant_id` and `intent`.
  - A regeneration queues `chat_regenerate` under the key `chat_regenerate:<variant id>`, with payload `message_id` and `variant_id`.
  - For P02.5c: the reply and regenerate handlers must fill the existing `streaming` variant named by `variant_id` (variant content, then message content), then set the variant and the message to complete, failed or interrupted. They must not insert a new variant, so a second generation overwrites the same variant.
  - Handlers are still not registered and the worker is not started in the app lifespan. A restart also leaves a variant `streaming` and the message `queued` or `streaming` until a handler runs.
  - In `ruff format`, a signature or assert that fits within 120 characters on one line gets joined, so check the length by hand. Run `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` to see the exact diff.
  - `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/integration/<file>.py ...` accepts several files and runs the integration files at the unit level.

## 2026-11-07T03:17:06Z - P02.5c
- Implemented P02.5c. New `events/hub.py` (EventHub behind a new `ports/events.py` EventPublisher port): process-wide event ids from 1, the newest 2000 events kept for replay, one bounded queue per connection (overflow becomes a single resync.required). `/ws` now answers the first `hello` with the replay, or with `resync.required` carrying the newest id when the boot_id differs, the id was never handed out, the gap left the buffer, or the hello is malformed; events flow only after the hello. New `services/chat_inference.py` ChatInferenceService handles chat_reply and chat_regenerate: it claims the payload's streaming variant (message -> streaming), builds the P02 prompt from `context/minimal.py` (blocks 1 and 2 from the card, recent complete/interrupted turns, trimmed with the runtime's own token counts) rendered by the P02.3b formatter, streams through the TextRuntime port publishing typing.started, message.delta (UTF-16 offsets), typing.stopped, message.completed/failed/updated, and saves text every 2 s and at the end in short transactions. A retryable failure puts the reply back to queued with empty text; a final failure stores failed with the partial text; a shutdown flushes and leaves it streaming. `recover_interrupted_replies` (startup step 5, before job recovery) marks streaming replies and their variants interrupted and fails their running jobs with JOB_INTERRUPTED. `main.create_app` builds the hub always and, with a `Worker` (KoboldCppRuntime + OrchestratorClient in main), runs JobScheduler in the lifespan; send and regenerate wake it via notify. Checks run: the 2 new test files (11 tests) passed in a targeted `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit` run; 83 existing tests in the affected files (test_app, test_import_rules, test_openapi_document, test_logs, test_local_only, test_messages, test_health_and_startup, test_job_queue) passed; `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed after fixing two docstrings that were over 120 characters. The full unit/integration/e2e/docker levels were not run.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/ports/events.py, backend/src/lyrveil/events/__init__.py, backend/src/lyrveil/events/hub.py, backend/src/lyrveil/api/events.py, backend/src/lyrveil/api/messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/domain/messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/domain/characters.py, backend/src/lyrveil/context/__init__.py, backend/src/lyrveil/context/minimal.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/conversations.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/chat_inference.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/tests/unit/test_event_hub.py, backend/tests/integration/test_reply_streaming.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/api.md, docs/backend.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/testing.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - `app.state.events` is the EventHub. It is not thread-safe: publish on the event loop; from a sync REST route use `anyio.from_thread.run_sync(hub.publish, ...)`. Publish only after the change is committed.
  - Only the reply job publishes events so far (typing.*, message.delta/completed/failed/updated). The REST routes publish no message.created/updated/deleted, and nothing publishes job.updated, conversation.updated or gpu.state yet.
  - The worker starts only when `create_app(..., worker=Worker(...))` is given and the lifespan runs. `TestClient(app)` without `with` never starts it, so existing tests are unaffected. `main()` passes `Worker(KoboldCppRuntime, OrchestratorClient(token))`, and `current_mode` always returns LLM until startup step 6.
  - `app.state.scheduler` holds the running JobScheduler (None otherwise); routes wake it with `_wake_worker` in api/messages.py.
  - ReplySettings hard-codes the gemma4 family and preset, max_length 400 and the user `Utente`; a settings store must replace it. `generation_params_json` and `checks_json` stay NULL.
  - To test the app with its worker on one event loop: `async with app.router.lifespan_context(app)` plus `httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=app))`, and the raw-ASGI `Socket` helper in tests/integration/test_reply_streaming.py for /ws. Every wait has an anyio deadline.
  - ruff E501 counts docstring lines too: two module docstrings of 121 and 122 characters failed `test lint`. `ruff format` joins any signature or call that fits in exactly 120 characters.
  - `websockets` is still missing from pyproject.toml and uv.lock, so the backend image cannot serve /ws yet.
  - In this session Bash refused `git status`; `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test ...` commands were allowed.

  - ruff format also reformats a long `assert (...) == (...)` tuple comparison into one element per line. Split such asserts into two statements.

- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - @angular/forms was not a dependency; `npm install --save-exact @angular/forms@22.2.4` added it. Signal Forms API: `form(modelSignal, schema)`, the `[formField]` directive, `validate(path, ctx => ({kind, message}) | null)`, `applyEach` for array items, and `schema(fn)` to build a schema. A custom control implements `FormValueControl<T>` with `value = model<T>()`. Its input names must not clash with the contract (`max`, `min`, `disabled`, `name`, `required`...); `max` made the compiler fail with NG8022, so the picker uses `limit`.
  - The validation message is on `ValidationError.message`. Rules are pure functions returning a catalog key and params (`Problem`), and the schema turns them into Italian text with the Translator. That keeps the same rules testable without a form.
  - Signal Forms does not set aria-invalid. The Creator uses `provideSignalFormsConfig({classes})` for a red border class, and `lyr-field` shows the message once the field is touched. `form().errorSummary()[0].fieldTree().focusBoundControl()` focuses the first invalid control.
  - The Creator renders all four tab panels and hides inactive ones with `hidden`, so tag-picker state and fields survive tab switches and Playwright cannot interact with a hidden tab's controls until its tab is clicked.
  - `GET /characters/vocabulary` is new and must stay declared before `/characters/{character_id}`. Like the other character routes it is not in backend/openapi.json, so the frontend keeps hand-written types in card.model.ts until the document is regenerated.
  - The starting seed is limited to Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER in the form because a JSON number loses precision above it; the server accepts up to 2**63 - 1.
  - Run one frontend spec file with `npm run test -- --include <glob>`. The `npm run test` script appends extra arguments to the `node --test` command too, so the whole unit suite ran anyway. `./scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/integration/test_characters.py` works from the repo root in Git Bash.
  - T-CHR-13 and its axe check are unverified. The next task that can run e2e should run tests/e2e/specs/character-creator.spec.mjs first and fix selectors or accessibility findings.

## 2026-11-07T05:33:48Z - P03.2b
- Built the character profile at /characters/:id and the backend for duplicate, archive, unarchive and purge. Backend tests, unit suites and lint pass. The new Playwright spec was written but never run, and the e2e acceptance for archive and purge is therefore unverified.

Checks I ran: `test lint` passes. `test unit` passes, with 76 frontend, 297 backend and 555 orchestrator tests plus the Pester tests. `tests/integration/test_characters.py` passes with 42 tests, and `tests/integration/test_conversations.py` passes with 7. I did not run the integration, e2e or docker suites in full.

**Backend**
- `POST /characters/{id}/duplicate` copies the current card into a new active character at version 1 with actor `duplicate`. The first name becomes "<name> (copia)", cut to stay within 80 characters. It copies no conversations, memories or older versions.
- `POST /characters/{id}/archive` and `/unarchive` are idempotent.
- `GET /characters/{id}/purge-preview` returns counts of conversations, messages and versions.
- `POST /characters/{id}/purge` takes `{"confirm_name"}` and returns 204.
  - It deletes, in one transaction, the jobs that name the character, its variants, messages, conversations, traits, visual profile, versions and the character itself.
  - A wrong name gives 422, a running job gives 409, and an unknown id gives 404. Nothing is deleted in any refusal.
- The main conversation list now leaves out conversations of archived characters. The profile still lists them when asked for one character.

**Frontend**
- The new profile screen shows the identity summary, traits with their locks, example exchanges, active conversations and the "simulated" note.
- **Nuova conversazione**, **Modifica**, **Duplica**, **Archivia** and **Rimuovi dall'archivio** work from the screen.
- **Elimina definitivamente** opens an inline confirmation with the preview counts. Its button stays disabled until the first name is typed, and the server checks the name again.
- The version list has **Confronta**, a field-by-field table against the current version, and **Ripristina**, which adds a new version.
- Switching to another id, as after a duplicate, loads that character in the same component.

**Tests added**
- T-CHR-04 and the archive, purge and running-job tests in `test_characters.py`.
- `character-profile-screen.spec.ts` and `version-diff.spec.ts`.
- `tests/e2e/specs/character-profile.spec.mjs`, which covers compare, restore, duplicate, archive and purge.

**Docs and changelog**
- Updated `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/api.md`, `docs/frontend.md`, `docs/character-system.md`, `docs/data-model.md` and `docs/testing.md`. I left the roadmap checkbox alone.

**Not verified or not delivered**

- Memories, images and assets do not exist yet, so purge removes no files and the preview has no counts for them.

  - The shell allowlist refuses `npm` and chained `cd && ...` commands. Use `./scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` and `./scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit [pytest path]` from the repo root, and the Read/Grep/Glob/Edit tools for everything else. `test unit` passes extra arguments only to pytest, so a frontend spec can only run through the whole unit level (about 1 minute).

## 2026-11-07T05:50:41Z - P03.3
- Assisted creation now works end to end. `POST /characters/drafts` checks the description (not blank, at most 2000 characters) and queues a `character_draft` job. The real JobScheduler runs that job under an llm lease, using `CharacterCreatorService` as its handler, registered in the app lifespan. The handler generates `CharacterDraft` under a GBNF grammar. That grammar is built from the schema at import by the new `schema_to_gbnf`. The output is checked first as a draft and then as a card with `validate_card`. If it is refused, the model gets one repair attempt with the Italian reason and its refused output in the prompt. A second refusal fails the job with STRUCTURED_OUTPUT_INVALID and that reason, and the job is not retried. A completed job's result is `{card, proposed_fields, character_id, attempts}` and no character is written. With `character_id`, `keep_locked` keeps the stored locked traits and visual fields, and the locks, seed, English slots and outfit tags are kept too; the stored card is not changed. A new `GET /jobs/{id}` returns a job's state and result but never its payload. In the Creator, a **Bozza assistita** panel queues a draft, polls that route every second showing the queue position, and fills the form without making it the saved state. A note lists the proposed fields by label. Only **Salva personaggio** goes on to save through the normal routes. A failed draft shows the server's reason.

Checks I ran:
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed after I fixed two ruff-format diffs and one mypy finding.
- `test unit tests/unit/test_character_draft.py`: 7 passed.
- `test unit tests/integration/test_character_drafts.py` (T-CHR-10 with fake_kobold over HTTP): 5 passed. This covered valid output, repaired output, repeated failure, locks over an existing character, and route refusals.

Not run: the two new frontend specs in `character-creator-screen.spec.ts`. They only run with the whole unit level, which I left to the orchestrator. The content guard on the description is not delivered (P03.6), and the docs say so.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/gbnf.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/structured.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/character_draft.py, backend/src/lyrveil/domain/card_draft.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/character_creator.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/card_rules.py, backend/src/lyrveil/api/jobs.py, backend/src/lyrveil/api/characters.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/tests/unit/test_character_draft.py, backend/tests/integration/test_character_drafts.py, frontend/src/app/features/characters/card.model.ts, frontend/src/app/features/characters/characters-store.ts, frontend/src/app/features/characters/character-creator-screen.ts, frontend/src/app/features/characters/character-creator-screen.html, frontend/src/app/features/characters/character-creator-screen.spec.ts, frontend/src/i18n/it.json, CHANGELOG.md, docs/api.md, docs/backend.md, docs/character-system.md, docs/frontend.md, docs/prompting.md, docs/testing.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test integration` takes no arguments, but `test unit tests/integration/<file>.py` runs one integration file with pytest in the backend container (the integration conftest applies), which is how T-CHR-10 was checked.
  - The backend has no Jinja dependency (adding one needs a uv.lock regeneration in Docker), so structured-task prompts are Python modules in lyrveil/prompts/; the .j2 names in docs/prompting.md are still planned files.
  - `generate_structured` (prompts/structured.py) and `schema_to_gbnf` (prompts/gbnf.py) are reusable for the other structured tasks (visual_profile_translate, extraction, summaries): pass a prompt builder taking `Repair | None` and a validate callback that raises OutputRejectedError with an Italian, value-free detail.
  - schema_to_gbnf raises UnsupportedSchemaError for free-key objects and floats; a model with such fields needs the converter extended first. It enumerates integer ranges of up to 256 values, so an unbounded int becomes any integer.
  - `card_rules._detail` is now public as `describe_error`, for draft validation errors.
  - The backend publishes no job.updated events yet; the Creator polls `GET /jobs/{id}` (api/jobs.py). When job events arrive, replace the polling in character-creator-screen.ts.
  - A draft over an existing character keeps the stored English identity slots even when the Italian text changes; the visual_profile_translate job (P05) must re-translate unlocked slots after such a save.
  - P03.6 (content guard) must add the description check to CharacterCreatorService.start_draft and the generated card check to its validate callback.
  - The two new Creator specs wait real time (DRAFT_POLL_MS + 50 ms) for the poll; they were not run in this change.

## 2026-11-07T07:10:47Z - P03.6a
- Implemented the content guard of docs/security.md (Content safeguards) and ADR 0018 for character data. New module backend/src/lyrveil/consistency/content_guard.py holds deterministic Italian and English lists and three per-sentence rules: minor terms (including a child's costume or act), ages 0-17 stated as an age (durations and age differences pass), and childhood next to a sexual term. Fixed childhood phrases about the character's own past ("da bambina", "a 16 anni", "as a child") pass unless a sexual term shares the sentence (ADR 0037). Text is normalized (case, accents, invisible characters, punctuation), and the term checks also read spaced letters joined and digit swaps undone. Entry points: validate_card (create, save, proposal, duplicate, import, draft output) plus CharacterService's two write paths (so restore and duplicate refuse old cards too); the draft description is checked before queuing and again in the handler before the model runs; a refused draft output gets its one repair, then the job fails with CONTENT_POLICY_BLOCKED. New ContentPolicyBlockedError (422 CONTENT_POLICY_BLOCKED, deliberately not a ValidationFailedError) names the field, never the text. The age rule stays in the schema and the DB CHECK. The lists are module constants; no setting, environment variable or file reaches them. Tests: unit file with every rule and pass case, every card text field, normalized list entries, and a no-configuration proof (AST import/name check, hostile environment); integration file for every API entry point, the DB CHECK on insert and update, and refusals unchanged under non-default settings, hostile environment variables and application_settings rows; draft tests for T-CHR-02 at the request, a queued job, and output repair or failure. Checks run: `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed; `test unit tests/unit/test_content_guard.py` 107 passed; `test unit tests/integration/test_content_guard.py tests/integration/test_character_drafts.py` 16 passed. The full unit and integration levels were not run. Because the unit and integration test files share a basename and files cannot be renamed or deleted with my tools, backend/tests/unit and backend/tests/integration became packages (__init__.py), which mypy required. Roadmap checkbox left alone.

**What now works**
- **Several conversations:** a character can have any number of conversations, and `GET /conversations?character_id=` lists them all. This already worked, and the new tests now cover it.
- **Scenario API:** revision `0009` adds the `scenarios` table. There is a scenario service and routes: `GET`, `POST /scenarios`, `GET`, `PUT`, `DELETE /scenarios/{id}`, and `POST /scenarios/{id}/archive` and `/unarchive`. Search ignores case and accents. Scenario text is checked with the same minor-content guard as character cards.
- **Conversation from a scenario:**
  - `POST /conversations` now accepts `scenario_id`. It returns 404 for an unknown scenario and 409 for an archived one or one made for another character.
  - The conversation stores a copy of the scenario and takes its language when none is given.
  - A non-empty opening message becomes the first message, written by the character.
  - The conversation's JSON also carries `scenario_id` and `scenario_title`.
- **Context isolation:** the reply prompt now includes a `Scenario:` block built only from that conversation's own copy, so editing or deleting the scenario later changes nothing in a running chat. An integration test starts three chats of one character, one from a scenario. It checks that no prompt contains another chat's messages and that only the scenario chat contains the scenario text.
- **Frontend:**
  - The `/scenarios` screen has a list with search, active and archived tabs, a create/edit form, archive/restore, delete with an inline confirmation, and **Inizia conversazione**.
  - **Inizia conversazione** goes to `/chats?scenario=<id>`, where the new-chat picker opens with that scenario chosen.
  - The picker has an optional **Scenario** choice, limited to the scenarios the chosen character can use.

**What is not covered or not verified**
- **Not run:** the Playwright spec `tests/e2e/specs/scenarios.spec.mjs` was written but not run.

- **T-CON-02:** the full test follows in P04. The scenario text in the conversation's context panel is not delivered.

## 2026-11-07T07:41:37Z - P03.7b
- Backend: new services/daily_backup.py (DailyBackup), started by the app lifespan from main(). Every 60 s it checks whether a backup is due: none in /data/backups was written on the current day in LYRVEIL_TZ, a backup of the command included. It writes one with cli.create_backup only while idle: no generation_jobs row is 'running', and PRAGMA data_version on a persistent read-only sqlite3 connection is unchanged since the previous check. It then prunes to the newest 7 with the new cli.prune_backups; the cli `backup` command prunes the same way. After a failure the next attempt waits 1 h. At startup it removes the `.lyrveil-*` leftovers of a backup cut by a stop. _lifespan was split into the backup task group and _job_worker, which keeps the old order: the scheduler is cancelled before the runtime closes. PowerShell: new scripts/lib/ToolContainer.psm1 holds the helpers moved out of DataFiles.psm1 (Get-ToolContainerArgument, Test-MountableFolder, Get-BackupFolder, Get-ToolTarget, Invoke-ToolContainer, Export-LyrveilBackup, which now returns an int exit status) plus the new Copy-BackupToWindows. Lifecycle.psm1 imports it, so start step 10 and stop step 3 copy the missing backups to the Windows folder, warn on any problem and never fail. Start, Stop, Restart and Restore take a test-only -BackupFolder. Tests: backend/tests/unit/test_daily_backup.py (9 tests), a new T-DCK-12 round-trip test in tests/docker/Data.Tests.ps1 with the helper tests/docker/round_trip.py (seed, change and read through the API and SQLite inside the backend container), a backup folder in the temporary drive for tests/docker/Lifecycle.Tests.ps1 so the real Documents folder is never touched, Data tests that no longer count backups, and a Copy-BackupToWindows case in tests/scripts/DataFiles.Tests.ps1. Checks run: `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit -k "daily_backup or cli"` passed (46 backend and 3 orchestrator tests); `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed after one ruff PT011 fix. NOT run: the docker level (T-DCK-12, the Lifecycle and Data tests) and the Pester unit tests. PSScriptAnalyzer did not check the untracked ToolContainer.psm1, and a direct analyzer run was denied. ## 2026-11-07T09:04:09Z - P04.2a
- Implemented the memory extraction pipeline of docs/memory-engine.md and docs/prompting.md. MemoryExtractionService (services/memory_extraction.py) is the memory_extraction handler, registered in the lifespan. The ExtractionResult schema (domain/memory_extraction.py) drives both the GBNF grammar and the validation. The prompt follows extraction.v1 (prompts/extraction.py) with one worked example, at temperature 0.3 and max_length 1200. Windows and triggers are in memory/window.py: up to 12 readable messages after last_extracted_seq, stopping before a pending reply, with 2 earlier messages as context and a window hash over message id, active variant id and content hash. A job is triggered after each stored reply, in its own transaction, when 10 messages are unprocessed (8 to 12 allowed), or 2 when a user message matches prompts/it/significant_patterns.txt. Only one extraction job runs per chat at a time. Idempotency: a run row is written running before generation. A completed window is never generated again; the cursor moves past it. A failed or interrupted run is restarted with attempt + 1. The commit transaction reads the window again and marks the run superseded if it changed; otherwise it writes the memories, sources (message, active variant, content hash), memory_fts rows, episodes with sources, links and episode_fts rows, the completed run with raw_output, and the cursor, then checks the trigger for what is left. Invalid output, refused twice, records the run failed with the refused text and raises a retryable STRUCTURED_OUTPUT_INVALID, so the job retries after 1 and 5 minutes; nothing else changes. Applied now: new and temporary inserts (inferences capped at 0.6; temporary items expire at the end of valid_until or after 7 days) and duplicate (adds sources, raises importance, no new version, a locked target gets only the sources); a new item with the same fingerprint as an existing memory counts as a duplicate. Deferred and counted only: update, contradiction and correction (P04.2b), relationship events and state (P04.6). Shared changes: gbnf ignores `format`; generate_structured returns the accepted text and StructuredOutputInvalidError.output holds the last refused text; memory.text gains fingerprint() and a max_terms cap on match_query. Checks run: `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed. `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test integration` passed (233 backend tests, including the 6 new ones; 200 orchestrator tests). `test unit -k "memory_extraction or memory_text or character_draft or job_policy or import_rules"`: 66 backend tests passed; the command reports FAILED overall only because the orchestrator part selected no test (pytest exit 5). `test unit -k "test_app or openapi or prompt_formatter or test_logs or koboldcpp"` passed (55 backend, 3 orchestrator). Not run: the full unit, e2e and docker suites.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/domain/memory_extraction.py, backend/src/lyrveil/memory/window.py, backend/src/lyrveil/memory/text.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/memory_extraction.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/extraction.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/it/significant_patterns.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/gbnf.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/structured.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/memory_extraction.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/chat_inference.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/tests/unit/test_memory_extraction.py, backend/tests/integration/test_memory_extraction.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/memory-engine.md, docs/prompting.md, docs/backend.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/api.md, docs/security.md, docs/testing.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - P04.2b plugs into services/memory_extraction.py: _apply_memory counts DEFERRED_RELATIONS (update, contradiction, correction) and writes nothing. Replace that branch, and the fingerprint-duplicate shortcut, with MemoryConsistencyService. The run, cursor and transaction framework (_prepare, _commit, superseded check) can stay as it is.
  - Items deferred before P04.2b are not recovered: their windows' runs are completed and the cursor has moved. Bumping PROMPT_VERSION or SCHEMA_VERSION only matters when a window is computed again, for example after the cursor moves back (P04.7 invalidation).
  - P04.6: relationship events and StateUpdate are validated (sources in the window) and counted as deferred_items; nothing writes character_state_history or relationship_states yet.
  - The trigger runs only after a reply is stored (ChatInferenceService._queue_extraction, its own BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction, with failures logged and swallowed). 'Ricorda questo', the idle flush and re-evaluation are not delivered.
  - lyrveil.memory.text.fingerprint hashes the sorted distinct stems, falling back to the normalized words for stop-word-only text. Episodes use the fingerprint of title plus description.
  - ruff format (0.17) joins implicit string concatenations that fit in 120 columns, so write a SQL string on one line when it fits.
  - `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit -k <filter>` reports FAILED when the filter selects no orchestrator test (pytest exit 5); read the backend part's result.
  - The P04.1 note asking for a purge test with real memory rows was already satisfied: tests/integration/test_memory_index.py seeds real rows and purges them.
  - mypy cannot infer `lambda mode=mode: mode`; use a small factory function returning a lambda instead.

## 2026-11-07T09:25:15Z - P04.2b
- Implemented the memory consistency rules of docs/memory-engine.md for extraction. A pure `decide` (backend/src/lyrveil/memory/consistency.py) decides what each extracted item does. MemoryConsistencyService (services/memory_consistency.py) reads its inputs and writes the result in the run's commit transaction, replacing the P04.2a deferred branch and the fingerprint shortcut.

Rules: deduplication by fingerprint and by stem-set Jaccard >= 0.8 (same kind, subject and value). update/correction supersede the target with valid_to, superseded_by_id, kept sources and a memory_revisions row; a correction with a user source is stored user_confirmed at 1.0. A contradiction makes both memories uncertain in one conflict_group_id and publishes memory.conflict at importance >= 3.

Guards: provenance check (an unsourced user fact becomes inferred; a character's self-statement becomes fictional); inference limit; deterministic single-valued subject conflict, using a new Italian change-cue list; locks (uncertain plus needs_review); temporary items never change lasting ones; identity_candidate changes nothing and is never shown. Temporary memories expire when a run starts, with a revision by `consistency`.

The extraction prompt is now extraction.v2: it labels uncertain memories `[incerto]` and excludes identity candidates. The job result adds memories_superseded, memories_uncertain and conflict_ids. main.py passes the event hub to the extraction service.

Checks run: `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/unit/test_memory_consistency.py tests/unit/test_memory_extraction.py tests/unit/test_memory_text.py tests/integration/test_memory_extraction.py tests/integration/test_memory_index.py` passed (70 tests). `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/unit/test_app.py tests/unit/test_prompt_formatter.py` passed (26). `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed. The full unit, integration, e2e and docker levels were not run.

Not covered: the 'Applica al profilo' half of T-CON-05 (P04.9); the reply-prompt half of T-MEM-10 and T-MEM-11, which needs retrieval and the context builder (P04.3, P04.5); tombstones (P04.2c).
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/memory/consistency.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/memory_consistency.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/it/change_cues.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/memory/window.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/memory_extraction.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/extraction.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/memory_extraction.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/tests/unit/test_memory_consistency.py, backend/tests/unit/test_memory_extraction.py, backend/tests/integration/test_memory_extraction.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/memory-engine.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/backend.md, docs/api.md, docs/prompting.md, docs/testing.md, docs/consistency-engine.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - P04.2c (tombstones) plugs into MemoryConsistencyService.apply in services/memory_consistency.py: check the tombstone before `consistency.decide` (or pass it in) and skip the insert when blocked. `memory_revisions` writing already exists (persistence/memory_extraction._revise), so a T-MEM-08 user edit can reuse it with actor='user'.

  - Extraction prompt is extraction.v2 (uncertain memories shown as `E2: [incerto] ...`). Bump TEMPLATE_VERSION for any further prompt text change.
  - Negations ('non') are stop words, so fingerprints ignore them: an update/correction never treats its own target as a duplicate; if the target stays in use, decide() confirms it to respect uq_memories_fingerprint.
  - A superseding insert must mark the old row superseded before inserting the new one (unique fingerprint index), and set superseded_by_id after (FK).
  - MemoryExtractionService takes `events: EventHub | None`; it publishes memory.conflict on the loop thread after the commit returns, never inside the worker thread.
  - Integration helper `Chat` in tests/integration/test_memory_extraction.py runs consecutive windows of one chat with scripted outputs; `Chat.shown()` returns the existing-memory lines of the last prompt (after the worked example, which also contains 'Studio Rossi').

## 2026-11-07T13:53:30Z - P04.2c
- Added user memory edits that record a revision and deletion that leaves a tombstone. Extraction now skips a candidate that a tombstone blocks. Both new integration tests and the new unit test pass, together with the neighbouring memory tests (28 in the last run), and `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passes. The full unit, integration, e2e and docker levels were not run. Nothing in the running app calls the new service yet: the `PATCH` and `DELETE /memories/{id}` routes belong to P04.9.

- **Edit:** `MemoryService.edit` in `services/memories.py` (SQL in `persistence/memories.py`) appends the earlier row to `memory_revisions` with actor `user` and reason `edit`, and moves the memory to the next version. It refreshes the full-text index row, keeps the sources and the lock, and writes nothing when nothing changes. It refuses a text that another memory in use already has, and an out-of-range importance.
- **Delete:** `MemoryService.delete` writes the tombstone and removes the memory, its revisions, sources and index row in one transaction. The tombstone keeps hashes and keys only, with no text. A memory with no source uses the conversation's extraction cursor as its newest-source position.
- **Tombstone check:** the check is `is_blocked` in `memory/consistency.py`. It runs after `decide`, so only an item that would store a new memory can be blocked. A candidate is blocked when it matches by fingerprint, or by subject and value hash, and every source is at or before the deleted memory's newest source or has a content hash the tombstone lists. A blocked item writes nothing and is counted as `blocked_items` in the job result.
- **Tests:** T-MEM-08 and T-MEM-09 are in `tests/integration/test_memory_extraction.py`, plus a unit test of `is_blocked`. The same-source block is tested by calling `apply` directly, because a normal extraction window never contains messages at or before the cursor.
- **Docs:** `memory-engine.md` (new "Delivered in P04.2c" section), `data-model.md`, `backend.md`, `api.md`, `testing.md` and `CHANGELOG.md`.
- **Not done:** routes (P04.9), tombstones for shared memories, and tombstones for episodes.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/memory/consistency.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/memory_extraction.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/memories.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/memories.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/memory_consistency.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/memory_extraction.py, backend/tests/integration/test_memory_extraction.py, backend/tests/unit/test_memory_consistency.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/memory-engine.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/backend.md, docs/api.md, docs/testing.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The Memory Studio routes (P04.9) should call `MemoryService` (`services/memories.py`) for `PATCH` and `DELETE /memories/{id}`. It raises NotFoundError, ValidationFailedError and ConflictError.
  - `persistence.memory_extraction.revise` is now public (it was `_revise`); `actor` is `extraction`, `user` or `consistency`.
  - Guard 6 runs after `consistency.decide` inside `MemoryConsistencyService.apply` and returns `Blocked()`. The extraction service counts it as `blocked_items` in the job result.
  - A window never holds a message at or before the cursor, and a completed run is skipped, so a normal extraction cannot re-read the deleted memory's source. Test the same-source case by calling `apply` directly. The real case arrives with invalidation (P04.7).
  - A tombstone's `value_hash` is the fingerprint of the value (`consistency.value_hash`), so a reworded value such as `studio rossi.` still matches.
  - `memories.delete` relies on cascades for `memory_revisions` and `memory_sources`. It removes the `memory_fts` row by hand, with no trigger.
  - Tombstones are only read and written for conversation memories. Shared-scope memories and episodes have none yet.

## 2026-11-07T14:10:14Z - P04.3
- P04.3 is implemented: `FtsMemoryRetriever` with the scoring and selection described in docs/memory-engine.md and ADR 0011.

- **Port:** `ports/memory_retriever.py` holds `RetrievalQuery`, `RetrievalResult` (items, token cost, candidate count, `.ids` for `previous_ids`), `RetrievedMemory`, `ScoreParts` and the `MemoryRetriever` Protocol.
- **Pure rules:** `memory/retrieval.py` holds `RetrievalWeights` (normalized), BM25 normalization per item type, the recency part with a 0.5 floor, the topic part, the kind and status factors, the locked and previous-reply bonuses capped at 1, the labels, line rendering, a deliberately high `estimate_tokens`, and `is_past_intent` (reads the new `prompts/it/past_cues.txt`). Selection does these steps in order:
  - sort by score;
  - keep one memory per subject (a superseded one under past intent sits beside the current fact);
  - add a conflict group whole or not at all;
  - cap at 6 per kind and 3 episodes;
  - skip any unit that does not fit the budget or `max_items`.
- **SQL:** `persistence/memory_retrieval.py` applies every hard filter in SQL: character; conversation, or the shared scope only when the query flag is set and the conversation itself has `shares_memory = 1`; status; expiry; no `identity_candidate`. Candidate routes: 64 FTS matches, 16 standing memories, the other conflict-group members, and 16 episodes. It also has `record_use`.
- **Service:** `services/memory_retrieval.py` runs one read transaction, then scores and selects. `record_use` updates `last_used_at` and `use_count`.

Tests:
- `tests/integration/test_memory_retrieval.py` covers T-MEM-05, T-MEM-15, T-CON-01, T-CON-02 and T-MEM-22, plus the use counters.
- `tests/unit/test_memory_retrieval.py` covers the pure rules.
- T-MEM-22 uses `backend/tests/fixtures/memory_retrieval/evaluation.json` (3 conversations, 60 questions). It is in `backend/tests/fixtures`, not the root `tests/fixtures`, because only `backend/` is copied into the test stage.
- Recall at 8 was 58 of 61 (95.1 %) against the 80 % target. The three misses are paraphrases I added on purpose to show ADR 0011's synonym limit; with direct word matches only it was 61/61.

Checks run:
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/unit/test_memory_retrieval.py tests/integration/test_memory_retrieval.py tests/unit/test_memory_text.py tests/integration/test_memory_index.py`: 49 passed.
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint`: passed.
- The full unit, integration, e2e and docker levels were not run.

Not delivered: the call from replies and the prompt block (P04.5), the `memory.retrieval.*` keys as user settings (weights are a constructor argument for now), and past-intent cues for languages other than Italian.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/ports/memory_retriever.py, backend/src/lyrveil/memory/retrieval.py, backend/src/lyrveil/memory/__init__.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/memory_retrieval.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/memory_retrieval.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/it/past_cues.txt, backend/tests/integration/test_memory_retrieval.py, backend/tests/unit/test_memory_retrieval.py, backend/tests/fixtures/memory_retrieval/evaluation.json, CHANGELOG.md, docs/memory-engine.md, docs/testing.md, docs/backend.md, docs/risks.md

  - The ContextBuilder (P04.5) should call `FtsMemoryRetriever(engine).retrieve_relevant_memories(RetrievalQuery(...))`. It passes `include_shared=conversation.shares_memory`, `past_intent=is_past_intent(latest_user_text, language)` and `previous_ids=` the `.ids` of the previous result. It calls `record_use(ids, now)` after the reply commits.
  - The token budget covers only the item lines (`RetrievedMemory.line` = `- <label> <content>`), not the 'Ricordi pertinenti:' header. The default cost is `estimate_tokens`, a high estimate (characters / 3 + 1). A runtime-backed cost can be injected through `token_cost=`.
  - The retrieval API is synchronous, so a runtime token count, which is async, must be cached or precomputed before it is injected as `token_cost`.
  - The evaluation fixture lives in `backend/tests/fixtures/` because the backend test stage copies only `backend/tests` and `tests/fakes/*.py` from the root.

## 2026-11-07T14:24:14Z - P04.4
- Implemented progressive summaries (backend only). New migration 0011 adds `conversation_summaries`, with a trigger that refuses any UPDATE except to `status`, so a checkpoint is never overwritten. `SummaryService` runs the `summary_checkpoint` job and does one step per run, in this order: re-summarize a range named in the payload (new version, old row `superseded`), summarize the next chunk, or write a rollup. Chunks leave out the newest 30 readable messages and take 40 messages or 2400 tokens, whichever comes first. The cursor moves in the same transaction as the checkpoint. Six consecutive checkpoints of a level merge the oldest four into one checkpoint of the next level, and the merged rows stay. The commit re-reads the covered messages; if they changed it writes nothing and ends the job `superseded`. `choose_summaries` implements "Choosing summaries for a prompt", but no prompt calls it yet (P04.5). The summary trigger is queued after each stored reply (next to the extraction trigger) and after each finished run. The handler is registered in the lifespan. Tests: T-MEM-16 and both halves of T-MEM-17 pass. The new files are 31 unit tests and 8 integration tests, and the migration tests were updated. `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed. The reply-streaming, memory-extraction and memory-index integration files also passed. The full unit, integration, e2e and docker levels were not run.
- Files changed: backend/migrations/versions/0011_summaries.py, backend/src/lyrveil/domain/summaries.py, backend/src/lyrveil/memory/summaries.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/summaries.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/summary.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/summaries.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/chat_inference.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/characters.py, backend/tests/unit/test_summaries.py, backend/tests/integration/test_summaries.py, backend/tests/integration/test_migrations.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/memory-engine.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/backend.md, docs/testing.md, docs/prompting.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The ContextBuilder (P04.5) should read `persistence.summaries.checkpoints(connection, conversation_id)` and call `memory.summaries.choose_summaries(checkpoints, recent_from_seq, budget)`. It returns a `Selection`: the checkpoints oldest first, plus the `gaps` to log in the prompt assembly. It then loads the text with `stored_summaries(connection, ids)`.
  - `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test integration` takes no arguments. To run a single integration file, pass it to the unit level: `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/integration/test_summaries.py`.
  - The next chunk depends on `persistence.summaries.tail_start`. It is the seq of the oldest of the newest N readable messages in the chat, or None if there are N or fewer. Anything at or after it is never summarized.
  - Invalidating checkpoints when a covered message changes is not delivered. Use `status = 'invalidated'`, which the trigger allows. A re-summarize job is a `summary_checkpoint` job with payload `{from_seq, to_seq}` for a level-0 range. Rollups that cover a re-summarized range are not recomputed. That is the next task's to design.
  - The summary token count comes from `runtime.count_tokens` inside the job. The chunk cost uses `estimate_tokens`, a high estimate that errs toward earlier chunks.
  - The `summary.chunk_messages` and `summary.recent_tail_messages` settings are `SummarySettings` constructor values (carried by `Worker.summaries`) until the settings store exists.

## 2026-11-07T14:50:54Z - P04.5
- Replies are now built by a full ContextBuilder.

**What a reply prompt holds now**
- **Stable text:** block 1, the full block 2 with traits, block 3 with its examples, the scenario copy, and block 6 with the chosen summary checkpoints.
- **History:** the messages after the newest active checkpoint.
- **Dynamic frame:** block 5 (memories from FtsMemoryRetriever) and the anchor (block 9).
- **Current message:** cut to 2048 tokens in the prompt only; the stored message is unchanged.

**Budget**
- The window is the context size the runtime reports.
- The reply reserve and the request's `max_length` come from the character's length band (60/120/240/400), capped by `llm.max_length`. The default card's band, `brevi`, now sends 120 instead of 400.
- Every count that decides comes from the runtime. Counts are cached in memory by text hash and model name.
- The whole prompt is counted with `special` after the fill and after each reduction round (at most 3 rounds).
- Reductions run in the documented order, each spent before the next starts.

**Choices the docs didn't fix (written up in memory-engine.md "Delivered in P04.5")**
- One extra last-resort step drops history below its 800-token floor, so a prompt never overflows the window and loses its global instructions.
- When the history was cut by the remaining room, an overshoot comes out of the history first, so a small miss in the turn prices never changes the stable text.
- `previous_ids` and token counts are kept in memory only. `messages.token_count` is not written.
- Block 2 gained "Dal tuo passato", "Interessi" and "Preferenze" lines. The reduction order names interests and background as prompt fields, and the old template lacked them.

After each reply, the use of its memories is recorded. The assembly is logged as counts only (`context_assembled`, plus `context_over_budget` when even the parts never removed don't fit).

**Checks I ran**
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/unit/test_context_builder.py`: 17 passed (T-CON-11, current-message cut, T-MOD-05 with the full context for each family).
- The same command with `tests/integration/test_context_builder.py`: 5 passed (T-MEM-21 at 8192 and 4096, runtime counts deciding, reduction order at 4096×3 and 1536, the service wiring and use counters).
- The formatter, summary, reply-streaming, scenario, extraction, retrieval, conversation and message files: 119 passed. Existing T-MOD-05 still passes.
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint`: passed.
- I did not run the full unit, integration, e2e or docker levels.

**Not delivered**
- The `prompt_assemblies` table. Data-model.md now places it with the P04.9 diagnostic mode, its first reader.
- Block 4 (needs P04.6 state and P07.4 time context).
- Corrective notes (P04.8). The anchor already takes them.
- The interface note for a cut message.
- The `context.*` keys as user settings.
- `context.reply_reserve_tokens` is not read, since the band sets the reserve. Whether to keep that key is recorded as open.

No injected instructions were found in the files I read.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/context/__init__.py, backend/src/lyrveil/context/blocks.py, backend/src/lyrveil/context/builder.py, backend/src/lyrveil/context/tokens.py, backend/src/lyrveil/context/minimal.py, backend/src/lyrveil/memory/summaries.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/summaries.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/chat_inference.py, backend/src/lyrveil/domain/characters.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/tests/unit/test_context_builder.py, backend/tests/integration/test_context_builder.py, backend/tests/integration/test_reply_streaming.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/memory-engine.md, docs/prompting.md, docs/backend.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/testing.md

## 2026-11-07T15:17:59Z - P04.6
- Implemented P04.6. `CharacterStateManager` (`services/character_state.py`) works on the pure rules in `consistency/state.py` and the SQL in `persistence/relationship.py`.

- **Replay.** The state is computed in one way only: a pure replay of the conversation's applied `character_state_history` rows, in write order (`created_at`, then rowid). Each relationship row stores its requested delta. The replay recomputes the limits: ±8 net per dimension per run, ±15 per local day in `LYRVEIL_TZ`, and a range of 0 to 100. So when rows are invalidated, they also stop counting against the limits of the rows that remain. `value_before` and `value_after` record what was applied at write time.
- **Stored state.** After every write or invalidation, `relationship_states` is rewritten from a replay of the rows read back from the database.
- **Large changes.** A `large` event counts as 8 only when the same run stored or confirmed an episode of importance 4 or more; otherwise it counts as 4.
- **Reasons and sources.** Every row has a reason and at least one source message; the `Proposal` type refuses anything else. Rows from the state update cite every message of the run's window and use a fixed reason for each kind.
- **Extraction.** `MemoryExtractionService` applies the relationship events and the state update (mood, threads opened and closed, goals, narrative changes) in the same transaction as the run. The job result gains `state_events`, and `deferred_items` now counts only a proposed `current_topic`. The extraction prompt is now `extraction.v3`: it lists the open threads so the model can close one by repeating its text.
- **Prompt.** `load_context_request` fills block 4 for any chat that has a state row. The bands are worded in the second person ("ti fidi di Sara"), because the documented table is written from the user's side. No numbers reach the prompt.
- **Invalidation.** `invalidate()` marks the rows citing given messages as invalidated and replays the rest. Nothing calls it yet; the hook from message edits and deletions is P04.7.

**Checks:** `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit` passed on the new and affected files (113 tests: the two new state files, plus the extraction, context-builder, reply-streaming, summary, index and migration files). `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed. I did not run the full unit, integration, e2e or docker levels.

**Not delivered:**
- `current_topic` has no event kind; adding one needs a migration of the CHECK list, which is still an open question.
- A scenario's starting state; scenarios have no such fields.
- User corrections and the relationship routes (P04.9).
- Goals and narrative changes are stored but not shown in the prompt, because the block 4 template has no line for them.

I found no injected instructions in the files I read.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/consistency/state.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/relationship.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/character_state.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/memory_extraction.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/chat_inference.py, backend/src/lyrveil/context/blocks.py, backend/src/lyrveil/context/builder.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/extraction.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/tests/unit/test_character_state.py, backend/tests/integration/test_character_state.py, backend/tests/integration/test_memory_extraction.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/consistency-engine.md, docs/prompting.md, docs/memory-engine.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/backend.md, docs/api.md, docs/testing.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - P04.7 should call `CharacterStateManager(zone).invalidate(connection, conversation_id, message_ids, now)` in the transaction of the message change. It marks the rows citing those messages `invalidated` and stores the replay of the rest. State rows store message ids only, not variant ids, so any edit, deletion or variant switch of a cited message invalidates the row.
  - The rows of a state update cite every message of the run's window, so a change to any window message invalidates the mood, thread, goal and narrative rows of that run.
  - `relationship_states` exists only after the first run that writes a history row. `load_context_request` shows block 4 only when the row exists; after a full invalidation the row stays, with the starting values.
  - `MemoryExtractionService(..., zone=...)` gets `app.state.settings.zone` in main.py. Tests pass a zone or default to UTC.
  - The job result has a new key, `state_events`. `deferred_items` now counts only a proposed `current_topic`, which has no `event_kind` yet: adding one needs a migration that rebuilds the CHECK list.
  - The extraction prompt is `extraction.v3`. `ExtractionPrompt.open_threads` lists the state's threads under 'Discorsi in sospeso:'.
  - `user_correction` rows are ignored by the replay. P04.9 must define their encoding (which field a text correction targets) and add it to `consistency.state.replay`.
  - Block 4 band phrases are in `context/blocks.py` `STATE_BANDS`, in the second person. The documented user-side table in consistency-engine.md is meant for the UI (P04.9).

## 2026-11-07T15:52:29Z - P04.7
- P04.7 is implemented. Editing, deleting, regenerating a message or switching its variant now updates what was derived from it, in the same transaction as the change. Re-evaluation then rebuilds what still holds.

**What happens on a message change** (`services/invalidation.py`, SQL in `persistence/invalidation.py`):
- **Memories.** A locked memory only gets `needs_review`: no revision, nothing else changes. A memory that still has another valid source loses the stale one and 0.1 of confidence, with a revision (`source_changed`). A memory with no source left becomes `invalidated` and keeps its sources.
- **Repairs (my own addition).** When a memory in use is invalidated, the last member left in its conflict group leaves the group. The fact it had superseded becomes active again, unless that would clash with a fingerprint or a single-valued subject already in use. So deleting "Ho cambiato lavoro… Verdi" brings "Studio Rossi" back.
- **Episodes** follow the same rule, without confidence. The state rows that cite the message are invalidated and the state is replayed.
- **Summaries.** Checkpoints covering the message are invalidated, and their level-0 range is queued to be summarized again.
- **Re-evaluation.** A `memory_reevaluation` job is queued for seq−6 to seq+6, cut to the extraction cursor. A regenerated reply queues its job once it is stored.

**Re-evaluation run** (`MemoryExtractionService`, now registered for this job kind):
- It reads the range without moving the cursor.
- An item with the fingerprint of an invalidated memory or episode brings that row back, with a revision (`reevaluation`) and the new sources.
- A relationship event already cited by a remaining state row is skipped. The mood/thread update is skipped when newer state exists.
- New age check in `decide`: an item older than the memory it would change is stored `superseded` instead. Without it, re-reading old messages would put newer facts in doubt.
- A window whose run completed before the job was queued is generated again. This is the one exception to "a completed run is never repeated".

**Checks run:**
- The new integration file (T-MEM-18, two T-MEM-19 cases, T-MEM-20, T-CON-04) plus the consistency unit file: 18 passed.
- The existing extraction, summary, message, reply-streaming, state, retrieval, context-builder, queue, scenario, app, import-rule and policy files: 157 passed.
- The OpenAPI document and contract tests: 5 passed.
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed.
- I did not run the full unit, integration, e2e or docker levels.

**Known limits** (written down in memory-engine.md):
- An invalidated rollup is rebuilt only if it was the newest of its level.
- With 13 readable messages in the range, the newest one is not re-read.
- Relationship events from invalidated rows outside the range are not proposed again.
- A locked episode has no `needs_review` column, so it is left as it is.

I found no instructions planted in the files I read.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/services/invalidation.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/invalidation.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/memory_extraction.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/memory_consistency.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/summaries.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/chat_inference.py, backend/src/lyrveil/memory/consistency.py, backend/src/lyrveil/memory/window.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/memory_extraction.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/relationship.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/summaries.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/queue.py, backend/src/lyrveil/api/messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/tests/integration/test_memory_invalidation.py, backend/tests/unit/test_memory_consistency.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/memory-engine.md, docs/consistency-engine.md, docs/backend.md, docs/api.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/testing.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - `MessageService` now takes `zone`; the routes pass `app.state.settings.zone`. It calls `InvalidationService.message_changed(connection, message, now)` inside the write transaction of edit, delete, regenerate and activate_variant. Pass it the message reloaded after the change.
  - A source row holds while its message is not deleted and `active_variant_id` equals the row's `variant_id` (`persistence.memory_extraction.VALID_SOURCE`). Only invalidated and locked memories keep stale source rows.
  - `queue_reevaluation` (services/memory_extraction.py) queues only when seq <= last_extracted_seq, with the range cut to the cursor. Payloads are `{"from_seq", "to_seq"}` in that key order, because `JobQueue.has_queued` compares the stored JSON text.
  - A re-evaluation result adds `state_skipped` and `state_update_skipped`. Every extraction result now adds `memories_reactivated` and `episodes_reactivated`.
  - `KnownMemory` has an optional `valid_from`, and `Decision` an optional `superseded_by` (the age check). `NewMemory` has `valid_to`. `RunScope` has `reevaluation`.
  - The P04.9 Memory Studio should surface `needs_review` (set on locked memories by invalidation) and the invalidated memories, and use the revision reasons source_changed, invalidated, replacement_invalidated, conflict_cleared and reevaluation.
  - To run a single integration file: `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/integration/<file>.py`.
  - To test a crash in the middle of a job: mark the job running with `queue.start`, then make the fake runtime raise a `BaseException` subclass so no handler catches it. After that, dispose the engine, open a new one on `database_path` and call `recover_interrupted_jobs`.

## 2026-11-07T16:19:25Z - P04.8a
- Added lyrveil/consistency/normalizer.py, a deterministic streaming implementation of rules N1 to N5 (ADR 0032), and the single chat reply streaming path ChatInferenceService._stream that feeds every token through it before anything is published or stored. Stages: N1 (leading label in any case, with optional ** and :, en/em dash or '- ', plus wrapping quotes decided within a 40-character window), N2 (headings, rules, fences, tables and ** markers), N3 (narration spans of three or more words: nessuna/leggera/libera), the N5 emoji half (only for emoji mai) and the N5 spacing half (closes gaps left by removals and collapses blank lines and trailing spaces); N4 runs in finish. Each stage reads one character at a time or decides only on facts that more text cannot change, so the result does not depend on chunking. The text is held back only while a later character can still change it, so the existing streaming tests pass unchanged. A finished variant now gets checks_json {"normalizer": {...}}. When N4 cuts a reply stopped by the length limit, message.completed carries the shorter text, which the client already uses to replace the streamed text. Checks run: scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/unit/test_reply_normalizer.py (41 passed), scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/integration/test_reply_streaming.py (7 passed, existing tests included), scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint (passed). The full unit, integration, e2e and docker levels were not run. No planted instructions found in the files read.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/consistency/normalizer.py, backend/src/lyrveil/consistency/__init__.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/chat_inference.py, backend/tests/unit/test_reply_normalizer.py, backend/tests/integration/test_reply_streaming.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/consistency-engine.md, docs/backend.md, docs/api.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/testing.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - ReplyNormalizer(NormalizerRules.for_card(card)): feed(token) returns the released text (often empty), and finish(finish_reason) returns NormalizedReply(text, tail, fired). Its checks() method returns {"normalizer": {...}}. normalize_reply(raw, rules, finish_reason) handles text known in one piece.
  - ChatInferenceService._stream(reply, request, normalizer, streamed) is the only path for a generated reply's text: it publishes deltas, flushes the released text and returns (NormalizedReply, finish_reason). P04.8b's corrective generation should call it again with a new request and a new ReplyNormalizer, and must publish message.restarted plus reset the offset and streamed list before doing so.
  - checks_json is written only by _finish, as {"normalizer": {rule: count}}. P04.8b should add its check hits and the regeneration count under new keys and keep the normalizer key.
  - V2 and V3 are still only stop sequences. A V2/V3 cleanup stage can be put before _Opening in ReplyNormalizer's stage tuple. Stages use the sentinel \x00 (_REMOVED) to mark removed text, so that _Spacing closes the gap.
  - The Write and Edit tools turn \uXXXX escapes into literal characters, including invisible ones such as the zero-width joiner. Use Python \N{NAME} escapes instead. Ruff RUF001 flags the en dash and the right single quotation mark in strings.
  - N1 holds text back only while the start can still be a label or an opening quote. Fake runtime replies that start with the character's name or with a quote will therefore produce fewer deltas.
