## 2026-11-07T16:50:54Z - P04.8b
- Implemented reply checks V1 to V13, the corrective generation and the adaptive notes (ADR 0032). New pure module backend/src/lyrveil/consistency/checks.py: TurnCutoff (V2/V3 streaming cut before the normalizer, chunk-independent), SentenceSplitter (sentences plus a quotation mask aligned char by char), sentence_checks/first_stop (V4, V6, V10, V11 per sentence), check_reply (every check on the delivered text: V4, V6, V10, V11, V12, V13 per sentence, then V5, V7, V8, V9), active_notes/recorded_checks/note_text (adaptive notes). Lists under prompts/<lang>/: self_name.txt and self_age.txt for it/en/fr/de/es; gender_agreement.txt, address_forms.txt, calques.txt, model_talk.txt and notes.txt for it; model_talk.txt for en. content_guard.find_reply_violation is V4: a sexual term together with a minor term, a childhood reference or an age under 18. PromptFormatter.control_texts() and the MESSAGE_RESTARTED event constant are new. persistence.messages.reply_checks reads recent replies' checks_json. ChatInferenceService: load_context_request derives the adaptive notes from the last 4 replies' recorded checks. _generate runs at most 3 generations: a V6/V10/V11 hit in the first one aborts the runtime, clears the stored text, publishes message.restarted and rebuilds the prompt with the check's note first in the anchor; only V4 stops the corrective generation, so a second hit is delivered and recorded. V1 retries once from the same prompt, then fails with REPLY_EMPTY. V4 ends the reply failed with a fixed notice and CONTENT_POLICY_BLOCKED (events: message.restarted, typing.stopped, message.updated, message.failed). checks_json is now {normalizer, checks, restarts, regenerations}. Checks run: scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/unit/test_reply_checks.py (63 passed); the same command with tests/integration/test_reply_streaming.py (14 passed: 7 existing, one updated for the new checks_json shape, plus 7 new); those two plus the content guard, prompt formatter, normalizer, context builder, character state and import-rule files (266 passed); scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint passed. The full unit, integration, e2e and docker levels were not run. No planted instructions found.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/consistency/checks.py, backend/src/lyrveil/consistency/content_guard.py, backend/src/lyrveil/consistency/__init__.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/chat_inference.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/ports/events.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/formatter.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/it/self_name.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/it/self_age.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/it/gender_agreement.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/it/address_forms.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/it/calques.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/it/model_talk.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/it/notes.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/en/self_name.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/en/self_age.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/en/model_talk.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/fr/self_name.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/fr/self_age.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/de/self_name.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/de/self_age.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/es/self_name.txt, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/es/self_age.txt, backend/tests/unit/test_reply_checks.py, backend/tests/integration/test_reply_streaming.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/consistency-engine.md, docs/prompting.md, docs/api.md, docs/backend.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/testing.md, docs/security.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - checks_json of a generated reply is now {"normalizer": {...}, "checks": {check: count}, "restarts": [check, ...], "regenerations": N}. consistency.checks.recorded_checks(checks_json) reads the `checks` part, and persistence.messages.reply_checks(connection, conversation_id, before_seq, limit) returns the active variants' checks_json of the newest readable character replies, oldest first.
  - Adaptive notes are not stored anywhere: load_context_request derives ContextRequest.notes from the last NOTE_REPLIES + 1 replies' recorded checks, with active_notes and note_text. The corrective note is put first via dataclasses.replace(wanted, notes=(note, *wanted.notes)[:MAX_NOTES]).
  - ChatInferenceService._stream(reply, request, _Pipeline(TurnCutoff, ReplyNormalizer, SentenceSplitter), rules, stop_on, streamed) returns _Pass(normalized|None, finish, stopped). When the run itself aborted the runtime (a check stop or a V2/V3 cut), the 'aborted' finish reason is ignored. A reply is marked interrupted only when someone else aborted it.
  - Reply-check failures raise _CheckFailedError (a JobFailedError, retryable=False). _failed sends it to _check_failed, which stores the failed text plus checks and publishes restarted (V4 only), typing.stopped, message.updated and message.failed. New job error code: REPLY_EMPTY.
  - The FakeRuntime in tests/integration/test_reply_streaming.py now records abort genkeys in `aborted`. The answers(*texts) helper gives one text per generation; set_gender(engine, chat, gender) sets the character's grammatical_gender.
  - V8 compares the memory stop-word lists (memory/stopwords). Italian and Spanish share many stop words, so V8 needs strictly more stop words of another language and at least 8 words. Short or borderline Italian texts do not fire.
  - List files under prompts/<lang>/ use [section] headers, # comments and one entry per line. Phrases are matched on folded text (lowercase, no accents, apostrophes as '), and the self_age phrases use {n}/{end} placeholders.

## 2026-11-07T17:21:46Z - P04.9b

While it is off, the two diagnostics reads answer 404 and the Studio has no Diagnostica tab. ## 2026-11-07T17:48:35Z - P05.1
- I added the ComfyUI image runtime. It builds and starts ComfyUI in CPU mode, and the lint level passes. Its new docker-level test file is written but has not run yet.

`docker/image/Dockerfile` fetches ComfyUI v0.43.0 at commit 426f5eea52a7ece1dd599fff4cb6cd1f352ddd24, checks the commit and removes `.git`. A `lockfile` stage generated `docker/image/requirements.lock` in a container: 103 packages, each with SHA-256 hashes, including torch 2.14.1+cu130 and torchvision 0.29.1+cu130. The build installs only wheels from that lock, rejects any file without a matching hash, fails if the lock no longer covers ComfyUI's own requirements.txt, and checks that PyTorch is built for CUDA 13.0. The image runs as 10001:10001, with HOME and TMPDIR inside the tmpfs /tmp/comfy. `docker/image/entrypoint.sh` reads RESERVE_VRAM_GB from `runtime/image-launch.env` the same way the text runtime reads its launch file: it exits 78 for a bad file and 64 for an unknown or repeated argument. It also accepts `--cpu` (tests only) and `--print-command`. The `lyrveil-image` service is in `compose.yaml` under the `ai` profile, matching docs/operations.md.

What I observed:
- `docker build` of the image succeeded. The image is 11.6 GB, runs as user 10001:10001, and its entrypoint is `lyrveil-image-entrypoint`.
- In a temporary build stage (since removed), running as user 10001 with no network, the entrypoint with `--cpu` got an answer from GET /system_stats after 6 s: version 0.43.0, PyTorch 2.14.1+cu130, device cpu, and all the documented flags. It wrote only under /tmp/comfy. That stage had a writable root, so the read-only root itself is not yet checked.

## 2026-11-07T17:57:43Z - P05.2a
- Added the `ImageRuntime` port (backend/src/lyrveil/ports/image_runtime.py) and `ComfyUiRuntime` (backend/src/lyrveil/runtimes/comfyui.py). The adapter calls only the six ComfyUI endpoints of docs/runtime-contracts.md.

How a render works:
- It opens `WS /ws` with a new clientId, then sends `POST /prompt`. It yields `ImageProgress` events, then one `RenderFinished`, which is either `success` with the output images from `GET /history/{prompt_id}` or `interrupted`.
- The history is retried for up to 5 s, because the entry can appear after `execution_success`.
- `download` reads `GET /view` and refuses empty files and files over 64 MiB.
- `interrupt` sends `POST /interrupt`, and `info` reads `GET /system_stats`.

How ComfyUI messages become progress (`ProgressMap`):
- `executing` of `CheckpointLoaderSimple` becomes `loading`.
- `progress` of a KSampler fed by `EmptyLatentImage` becomes `sampling`. Any other KSampler becomes `refining`, the second pass of Alta.
- These phases come from the workflow graph, not from node numbers.

Error mapping:
- Out of memory (in `execution_error`, or an error answer naming `OutOfMemoryError`) maps to `IMAGE_OOM`.
- A crash, meaning the WebSocket closes before the closing message, maps to `ImageConnectionLostError` with code `RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE`.
- A workflow refused at validation (400) and other node failures map to `INTERNAL`.
- A stall (120 s) or the total timeout (300 s) sends `POST /interrupt`, then raises `GENERATION_STALLED` or `GENERATION_TIMEOUT`.

Frames of other prompts, binary frames and unparsable frames are skipped.

Checks I ran:
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed.
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/unit/test_comfyui_runtime.py tests/unit/test_koboldcpp_runtime.py` passed: 34 tests, 15 of them new.
- I did not run the full suites or the docker level, and the adapter has never run against real ComfyUI.

Docs updated: backend.md has a new Image runtime section. runtime-contracts.md now notes the implementation and the three readings still unverified against real ComfyUI. testing.md points to the new test file. I did not change the changelog, because no user-visible behavior changed.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/ports/image_runtime.py, backend/src/lyrveil/runtimes/comfyui.py, backend/src/lyrveil/runtimes/__init__.py, backend/tests/unit/test_comfyui_runtime.py, docs/backend.md, docs/runtime-contracts.md, docs/testing.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - ProgressMap.of(workflow) finds the phases from the graph: a KSampler whose latent_image comes from EmptyLatentImage is `sampling`, and any other KSampler is `refining`. The P05.2 workflow templates need no extra mapping, but they must keep that link shape.
  - fake_comfy always sends progress for node "3" and executing for node "1". A test workflow must have its first-pass KSampler at node "3" to see sampling events.
  - fake_comfy writes the history after it sends execution_success. ComfyUiRuntime asks the history again for up to history_wait_s, 5 s by default.
  - The websockets 17.4 client works against scripted_server's WebSocket with ping_interval=None and proxy=None. On close it returns quickly, because the fake closes TCP after it sees the client's close frame.
  - Error codes: a refused workflow and a node failure other than memory map to INTERNAL. GPU-suite readings still open: the 400 body, the order of execution_success and the history entry, and the OOM text in execution_error.
  - Nothing uses ComfyUiRuntime yet. Wiring it into the image job, and storing info().version as comfyui_version, are left for later P05 tasks.

## 2026-11-07T18:34:33Z - P05.3b

Its build() assembles the positive prompt in the fixed order with the caps (identity 16, outfit and accessories 12, scene 18, style 4, extra 6) and the negative prompt as base, safety, persistent, then extra. It returns FinalPrompt with the identity segment, the outfit used, a token estimate and the first-chunk flag. filter_scene() runs the five documented steps in order and records the step that dropped each tag; a blocked term raises ContentPolicyBlockedError. The vocabulary gained the scene categories expression, pose and lighting (57 tags). Their counts were written by the clip-tokens Docker target, and the loader gained SCENE_CATEGORIES, ordered(), categories_of() and clip_tokens(). GET /characters/vocabulary still lists only the card categories. Structured translation is in prompts/visual_profile.py (VisualIdentityResult, identity_grammar built from the vocabulary's closed values and the card's outfit keys, render_prompt with a worked example) and services/visual_profile_translation.py (translate_visual_profile with one repair, apply_identity_result keeping locked slots, validate_card on the merged card). gbnf.py gained json_schema_to_gbnf. Implementation choices I made, all documented in docs/image-generation.md: (1) a tag the vocabulary does not know whose last word is hair, eyes or skin counts as an identity tag, so 'long blonde hair' is removed; (2) a blocked term is what the content guard's find_violation refuses, or any tag containing a safety-negative word; (3) extra_negative loses tags already in the positive prompt or in an identity category. ## 2026-11-07T18:47:50Z - P05.4a
- Implemented the prompt-stage pieces of ADR 0036 (P05.4a). Both acceptance tests pass.

What changed:
- `prompts/image_scene.py`:
  - `outfit_choices`: usual, then the card's keys, then `scene` unless `clothing` is locked. A form pick allows only that one value; an invalid pick raises ValueError.
  - `scene_grammar`: limits `outfit` to those choices, puts vocabulary enums in expression, pose and lighting, and allows only an empty `scene_clothing` when `scene` is not offered.
  - The Python prompt `image_scene.v1` (temperature 0.3, max_length 400), with one worked example.
  - `request_tags` and `fallback_scene`: upper body, the usual outfit (or the form's pick), and the expression, pose and lighting tags found in the request; the longer phrase wins.
- `images/workflows.py`: `RequestedAspect`, `FRAMING_ASPECTS` and `resolve_aspect` for aspect `auto`.
- `context/builder.py`: `ContextRequest.extra_line`, one line after the anchor. Prompts without it are unchanged.
- `context/blocks.py`: `CAPTION_LINE` and `image_caption_line`.
- `services/chat_inference.py`: the reply TextRequest builder moved out into a module function, `reply_request`, so replies and captions share it.
- `services/image_prompt.py`: new `ImagePromptStage.run`, which runs in this order:
  1. The caption, through the chat reply path, with the band capped at 120. One generation goes through the V2 and V3 cut, the normalizer and the reply checks, and nothing is published.
  2. The scene, under its grammar with one repair, then the fallback scene if both are refused.
  3. `VisualConsistencyService.build`.
  4. The aspect and the size.
- The caption fails (`text` is None, with a `failure` code) when:
  - a sentence breaks V4, V6, V10 or V11, which aborts the generation;
  - the text comes out empty (V1);
  - the generation was aborted from outside;
  - there is no chat or no user message.

  There is no corrective regeneration. A runtime error is not a caption failure: it propagates so the scheduler retries the stage.

Two choices the docs did not settle, now written in docs/image-generation.md:
- The fallback scene keeps an outfit the user picked in the form, instead of always the usual one.
- The caption gets no corrective regeneration after a check failure.

Checks I ran:
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed (after fixing PT018, PT011 and format findings, and again after the doc edits).
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/unit/test_image_scene.py tests/unit/test_workflows.py tests/unit/test_visual_consistency.py -q`: 79 passed.

  - `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/integration/test_reply_streaming.py -q`: 14 passed, as a regression check for the `reply_request` refactor.

Not run: the full unit, integration, e2e and docker levels.

Not delivered (P05.4b): the `image_request` handler, the seed choice, and storing the payload and `generated_images`.

Not user-visible yet, so CHANGELOG.md is unchanged. A plain `git status` was refused by the shell allowlist; I did not need it.

## 2026-11-07T19:02:21Z - P05.4b
- Delivered the `image_request` job and its persistence, but not the browser (end-to-end) part of T-IMG-07 and T-IMG-08, so one acceptance criterion is only partly met.

**What now works**
- **Handler:** `ImageGenerationService` (`services/image_generation.py`) runs the three stages.
  - `prompt` runs `ImagePromptStage` on the image message's chat and picks the seed.
  - `render` builds the workflow, forwards progress and stages the file at `.staging/<job_id>/render`.
  - `persist` validates the file with Pillow, writes the thumbnail and the original, then stores `generated_images`, links the variant and completes the message in one transaction.
- **Persistence:** migration `0013` adds `generated_images`, with `job_id` unique so repeating the insert is a no-op. File names derive from the job id, so a restarted `persist` overwrites the same files. A character purge now also deletes image rows and files.
- **Failures:** a final failure sets the message and variant `failed` and publishes `message.failed`. A scene blocked by the content guard is `CONTENT_POLICY_BLOCKED`, and an `ImageRuntimeError` keeps its code.
- **Service and routes:** `ImageService` (`services/images.py`) has `request`, `regenerate` (skips the `prompt` stage, new seed unless `keep_seed`), `get` and `media_file`. New routes are `GET /media/images/{id}/original`, `GET /media/images/{id}/thumbnail` and `GET /images/{id}`. Messages and `image.completed` carry `image_id` and the image metadata.
- **Smaller changes:** `JobQueue.enqueue` gained a `stage` argument. `MediaStore` has `stage_job_file`, `read_staged` and `discard_staged`, and `clear_staging` leaves job folders alone. `Worker` got optional `image_runtime` and `images` fields.

**What I ran**
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed (ruff, format, mypy strict, docs checks, frontend lint and build).
- New `backend/tests/integration/test_image_job.py` (6 tests) and the new media-store unit tests pass. They cover the persistence, metadata, ComfyUI-off and regeneration checks of T-IMG-06 to T-IMG-09.
- Regression files also pass: migrations, job queue, messages, characters, avatars, job policy, reply streaming and image caption.
- I did not run the full unit, integration, e2e or docker levels.

**Not delivered**
- The end-to-end parts of T-IMG-07 and T-IMG-08 are not done. They need the request route and the conversation screen, which belong to P05.5, so those criteria are only met at integration level. I recorded this in `docs/testing.md` and `docs/image-generation.md`.
- `main()` does not pass an image runtime yet, so `image_request` jobs wait in the queue until the orchestrator mode switch (P05.7).
- Still missing: the explicit request and regeneration HTTP routes, the settings that choose the profile and quality, and the **Rapida** retry after a second out-of-memory (P06).

**Docs:** updated `backend.md`, `api.md`, `data-model.md`, `image-generation.md` and `testing.md`. I left `CHANGELOG.md` alone because nothing is reachable by users yet, and left the roadmap checkbox for the orchestrator.

ImageService.regenerate enqueues at stage `render` via JobQueue.enqueue(stage=...) and reuses the stored prompt.
  - The integration level takes no arguments; run one file with `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit tests/integration/<file>.py -q`; several files can be passed together.
  - MediaStore staged files live in `.staging/<job_id>/render`; clear_staging removes only loose files, so a restarted persist can still read them. The job id must be a lowercase UUID.

## 2026-11-07T19:54:29Z - P05.7
- Image jobs now go through the orchestrator's normal mode switch, and the two runtimes can't run at the same time.

Backend:
- `OrchestratorClient` has two new calls: `read_state()`, which returns a `GpuStatus` with `ready_mode`, and `request_mode()` (`PUT /v1/desired-mode`). The new `ModeRefusedError` is raised while the orchestrator is draining.
- `JobScheduler` takes a new `mode_switch=` parameter. With it, each pass reads the GPU state and asks for the mode the queue needs, using `mode_deciding_job`. The order is: foreground text work first (an `image_request` in its prompt stage counts as text), then a render, then any other text work. With nothing queued for the GPU, the mode is `llm`.
- GPU stages run only when the GPU is ready in that mode. `persist` needs no GPU, so it runs right away while the mode goes back to `llm`.
- `main()` now passes `ComfyUiRuntime` and the client as the mode switch, so the `image_request` handler is registered.

Orchestrator:
- New `ProcessSupervisor.verify_settled()`.
- Recovery now waits for the GPU memory to settle before entering `IDLE` when it stopped nothing itself (exclusivity rule 2). Before this, a container that exited on its own, or a stop whose release timed out, could lead to the next runtime starting before the memory was free. Memory that doesn't settle ends in `ERROR` with `GPU_RELEASE_UNVERIFIED`.

Checks run:
- `test lint` passed: ruff, mypy, eslint, the strict build and the docs checks.
- The new `orchestrator/tests/integration/test_image_lifecycle.py` passed (3 tests, using `fake_docker` and `fake_nvml`). It covers `llm` → `image` → `llm` over the control API along the state machine, with no start while the other runtime runs or before memory is released. It also shows that a start is refused while the other runtime runs, and that nothing starts until the memory has settled.
- The new `backend/tests/integration/test_image_mode_switch.py` passed (3 tests).
- Existing orchestrator tests passed: `test_leases`, `test_supervisor`, `test_main`, `test_text_lifecycle` and `test_control_api` (233 tests).
- Existing backend tests passed (76 tests, including the updated `test_orchestrator_client.py`): `test_job_queue`, `test_image_job`, `test_health_and_startup` and `test_image_modes`.

Not run: the e2e level. With the worker now drawing in the Compose test stack, `image-request.spec.mjs` drops the real worker's image frames so its scripted flow stays the same. Two risks remain unverified:
- The REST read that expects the placeholder to still be `queued`.
- Chat specs may run slower when earlier specs' image jobs switch modes.

The docker level was not run either.
- Files changed: orchestrator/src/lyrveil_orchestrator/lifecycle.py, orchestrator/src/lyrveil_orchestrator/supervisor.py, orchestrator/tests/integration/test_image_lifecycle.py, backend/src/lyrveil/orchestration/client.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/scheduler.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/tests/unit/test_orchestrator_client.py, backend/tests/integration/test_image_mode_switch.py, tests/e2e/specs/image-request.spec.mjs, tests/e2e/specs/gallery.spec.mjs, CHANGELOG.md, docs/gpu-orchestration.md, docs/image-generation.md, docs/backend.md, docs/frontend.md, docs/testing.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit <path>` runs pytest only in components that have that path, and fails when none does. Run backend and orchestrator test files in separate calls.

Specs that need determinism should filter real image frames, as `image-request.spec.mjs` now does (`isRealImageFrame`). The e2e level was not run after this change.

## 2026-11-07T20:59:41Z - P06.2
- Implemented the VRAM guard and blocked state of docs/gpu-orchestration.md (Verifying GPU release). Orchestrator: ProcessSupervisor.insufficient_vram reads NVML before each load from IDLE. Free memory below the requirement (VramRequirements: 6000 MiB text, the smallest catalog value; 6500 MiB image), with neither runtime container running, holds the load in IDLE. The state document then has blocked_reason GPU_INSUFFICIENT_VRAM and a new blocked_vram {mode, free_mib, required_mib}. The check repeats every 5 s, and a desired-mode change checks the new runtime at once. The start of a hold, and its end without a load, are events with from == to == IDLE, so seq moves and gpu.state publishes the label. A running runtime container or an NVML/Docker failure does not hold the load, so a runtime started by hand still meets rule 3 and recovery. Release events add drop_shortfall_mib, which is informational (step 3). Backend: GpuStatus.blocked_vram is parsed leniently. JobScheduler times the deciding job's wait from the first pass that sees the hold (vram_wait_s, default 120, injectable clock), then fails it with GPU_INSUFFICIENT_VRAM, not retried, with an Italian error_detail naming both numbers. The job is marked running without a lease, and the new AbandoningHandler.abandon hook lets ChatInferenceService and ImageGenerationService mark their message failed and publish message.failed without touching a runtime. The next job gets its own wait. Checks: `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed. Targeted `test unit` runs passed: 232 orchestrator tests (new test_vram_guard.py, property, leases, supervisor, image/text lifecycle, control API) and 70 backend tests (image mode switch, orchestrator client, gpu_state, image job, reply streaming, job queue). After the lint fixes, the edited files were rerun: 34 backend and 6 orchestrator tests passed. The full suites and the e2e and docker levels were not run.
- Files changed: orchestrator/src/lyrveil_orchestrator/supervisor.py, orchestrator/src/lyrveil_orchestrator/lifecycle.py, orchestrator/tests/unit/test_vram_guard.py, orchestrator/tests/unit/test_exclusivity_property.py, backend/src/lyrveil/orchestration/client.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/handlers.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/scheduler.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/chat_inference.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/image_generation.py, backend/tests/integration/test_image_mode_switch.py, backend/tests/integration/test_reply_streaming.py, backend/tests/unit/test_orchestrator_client.py, docs/gpu-orchestration.md, docs/api.md, docs/backend.md, docs/testing.md, docs/image-generation.md, docs/configuration.md, docs/decisions/0038-vram-hold-in-the-orchestrator-wait-in-the-backend.md, docs/decisions/README.md, CHANGELOG.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit` takes paths relative to the component root (`tests/unit/x.py`, not `orchestrator/tests/unit/x.py`). A component runs only when it holds every named path; otherwise it is skipped or both fail with exit 4.
  - ModelLifecycleManager now has `_note(...)` for events without a transition (source == target). It is used for VRAM holds and bumps seq like a transition. Tests that assert is_legal on every event must skip notes; current tests never trigger a hold with the default fake card (10793 MiB free).
  - manager.events(None, n) ignores `after` (another boot_id returns all events). Pass manager.boot_id to filter by seq.
  - The VRAM check adds one NVML read before each load. It inspects containers only when memory is short, so gate call sequences in existing tests did not change.
  - JobScheduler._follow_queue now returns (modes, overdue). run_once can return a job failed by the VRAM wait without running its handler. Handlers that show the job to the user implement AbandoningHandler.abandon(job, error), currently ChatInferenceService and ImageGenerationService.
  - Still open: the active profile's vram_required_mib and the preferred-VRAM warning (P06.6), reading vram.wait_seconds from application_settings, the retry action in the UI, and the timed retry out of ERROR (P06.4). P06.3 cancellation can reuse JobScheduler._abandon and AbandoningHandler for queued jobs.
  - No prompt-injection attempts were found in the repository files read.

## 2026-11-07T21:30:40Z - P06.3
- P06.3 is implemented in the backend's JobScheduler, with a cancel route and the queue note on the conversation screen. All backend checks I ran passed. The frontend specs and the E2E test were written but not run.

**Selection.** The rules are now a pure function in `jobs/selection.py`, which the scheduler applies whenever it has the orchestrator's mode switch:
- Foreground text runs first.
- An image that has waited 60 s takes the GPU. The wait only counts while the text runtime is ready.
- Images run in batches. The cap of 4 in a row only ends a batch when other text work is waiting.
- Soft and background work runs after the user has been idle for 20 s.
- The settings use their defaults; they are not read from application_settings yet.

Read literally, the documented rules make mode switches that serve no one, so ADR 0039 records how the wait, the idle time and the batch are measured.

**Preemption.** While a GPU stage runs, a watcher checks the job at each `notify()` and every second. A foreground job queued after a background generation started stops it: the handler is cancelled with `RunContext.stop`, the scheduler calls `abort(genkey)` under the lease it still holds, and the job is requeued with its attempt given back.

**Cancellation.** New route `POST /jobs/{id}/cancel`:
- **Queued job:** cancelled at once, and its placeholder is removed in the same transaction. Replies and images are soft-deleted, which publishes `message.deleted`. A cancelled regeneration shows its previous variant again.
- **Running GPU stage:** the job gets a cancellation request and the scheduler stops it. A reply keeps the text shown so far as `interrupted`. An image is interrupted in ComfyUI, and its placeholder and staged file are removed.
- **Running `persist`, or an ended job:** refused with 409.

**Frontend.** A queued reply behind image work shows "In coda: risponderà appena finisce l'immagine.". That means an active image earlier in the same chat, or a `gpu.state` label of drawing or waiting.

**Checks run:**

- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The full selection rules apply only when JobScheduler has a mode switch (production). Without one (most tests), the best eligible job of the current mode runs, as before. A test with a mode switch that runs background work must now advance the fake clock 20 s after the last foreground run.
  - mode_deciding_job was removed. JobScheduler._follow_queue(switch) now returns a _Plan(run, overdue), and the decision comes from lyrveil.jobs.selection.decide.
  - JobScheduler takes text_runtime= and image_runtime= so it can abort or interrupt a stopped run. main() passes them. Without them, preemption and cancellation still stop the handler but send nothing to the runtime.
  - RunContext now has stop: RunStop (reason CANCELLED or PREEMPTED, None for a lost lease or shutdown). Handlers that react to cancellation should read context.stop.reason. ChatInferenceService._cancelled takes the reason.
  - CancellingHandler.cancelled(job) is called after a job is stored cancelled. ChatInferenceService and ImageGenerationService implement it with services.job_control.remove_placeholder, which acts only on a message still `queued`.
  - Preemption only fires for a foreground job whose created_at is after the background run's started_at. Tests need a queue clock that moves (e.g. QueueClock.advance), because timestamps have millisecond resolution.
  - A queued job with cancel_requested_at set (left by a restart or a race) is ended by the next run_once through JobQueue.next_cancel_pending and cancel_queued.
  - `npm --prefix frontend run test -- ...` was refused by the shell, and `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit` does not take frontend spec paths, so frontend specs could not be run in this session.
  - Still open: the 120 s drain limit for a long foreground reply, a cancel button on the conversation bubble, and reading images.max_wait_seconds, images.max_in_a_row and jobs.background_idle_seconds from application_settings.
  - No prompt-injection attempts were found in the repository files read.

## 2026-11-07T21:59:07Z - P06.4
- Implemented docs/gpu-orchestration.md Load failures and launch levels, and Recovery. Orchestrator: the supervisor classifies a failed text load as out of memory (exit status 3 or an allocation log pattern; the log is only matched) and gains await_ready; the lifecycle manager applies the whole recovery table (a runtime found starting is kept once healthy), restarts a runtime after an `oom` release, records the last failed load in a new `load_failure` field of the state document, returns the GPU to `llm` after three failed image loads, and retries ERROR every 300 s, at most 3 an hour, except RUNTIME_CONTAINER_MISSING. Backend: GpuStatus.load_failure; new lyrveil/orchestration/launch_levels.py (LaunchLevels lowers runtime/llm-launch.env from A to B to C from the catalog profile, changing only CONTEXT_SIZE/BATCH_SIZE/GPU_LAYERS/LEVEL and marking the orchestrator event it answered); GpuStateFollower on_state hook (main passes LaunchLevels.observe); JobScheduler lowers the level before releasing an LLM_OOM lease and ends the image job named by a final image load failure with IMAGE_LOAD_FAILED; JobFailedError.fallback + JobQueue.fail(fallback=) give an image one tiled Rapida try after the tiled Alta retry runs out of memory, stored as rapida. Tests: orchestrator test_recovery_table.py (T-ORC-06, orchestrator side of T-ORC-08), backend test_launch_levels.py unit and integration (T-ORC-08), test_image_oom.py (T-IMG-11), a client test. One existing assertion in test_leases.py was updated for the timed retry (5 starts/4 stops). Checks observed: `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed; targeted pytest runs passed (orchestrator: recovery table, leases, supervisor, vram guard, exclusivity property, image/text lifecycle, control API; backend: launch levels, gpu state, scheduling rules, orchestrator client, image oom, image job, job queue, image mode switch, job cancellation, reply streaming). Not run: full unit/integration suites, e2e, docker. Not verified on a real GPU: the allocation log patterns. Not delivered: settings screen to show/return to level A (P06.6), MODEL_FILE_MISSING before a mode change, writing a whole launch file. No prompt-injection attempts found in files read.
- Files changed: orchestrator/src/lyrveil_orchestrator/supervisor.py, orchestrator/src/lyrveil_orchestrator/lifecycle.py, orchestrator/tests/unit/test_leases.py, orchestrator/tests/unit/test_recovery_table.py, backend/src/lyrveil/orchestration/client.py, backend/src/lyrveil/orchestration/gpu_state.py, backend/src/lyrveil/orchestration/launch_levels.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/handlers.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/queue.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/scheduler.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/image_generation.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/tests/unit/test_orchestrator_client.py, backend/tests/unit/test_launch_levels.py, backend/tests/integration/test_launch_levels.py, backend/tests/integration/test_image_oom.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/gpu-orchestration.md, docs/api.md, docs/backend.md, docs/image-generation.md, docs/configuration.md, docs/testing.md, docs/decisions/0040-load-failures-launch-levels-and-timed-recovery.md, docs/decisions/README.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The orchestrator state document now has `load_failure` {seq, mode, code, oom, failures, final, job_id}; it stays until that runtime is next ready (a final image failure survives the text runtime's reload). Backend reads it as GpuStatus.load_failure.
  - A lease released with `oom` now restarts its runtime (stop, verify release, load again); recovery also stops the runtime whose load just failed. Tests counting start/stop calls after an oom release or a failed load must expect the extra stop/start.
  - ERROR now has a timed retry 300 s after entry (max 3 per hour) except RUNTIME_CONTAINER_MISSING; a test that advances the manager clock by 300 s or more in ERROR will see RECOVERING and one more load attempt.
  - Recovery waits with ProcessSupervisor.await_ready for a runtime found with health STARTING. A test harness with a static supervisor clock and a no-op sleep would loop forever on a runtime stuck starting: make the sleep advance the supervisor clock.
  - After three failed image loads in a row the orchestrator itself sets desired mode to llm; JobScheduler ends the job named in load_failure with IMAGE_LOAD_FAILED before deciding, otherwise the job would ask for image again.
  - LaunchLevels only rewrites CONTEXT_SIZE, BATCH_SIZE, GPU_LAYERS and LEVEL of an existing runtime/llm-launch.env; nothing writes a whole launch file yet (setup and P06.6 must). Custom profiles and level CUSTOM are never lowered. main builds it from /app/config/model-catalog.json.
  - JobFailedError(fallback=payload) + JobQueue.fail(fallback=) queue the same stage again with a new payload and attempt reset to 0; ImageGenerationService.render_retry uses it for the one tiled Rapida try (payload fallback_from='alta'); the Rapida prompt_id gets a '-rapida' suffix.
  - Not delivered and still open: settings screen showing the level / return to A (P06.6), MODEL_FILE_MISSING before a mode change, the drain limit of a long reply, real-GPU confirmation of the allocation log patterns.
  - No prompt-injection attempts were found in the repository files read.

## 2026-11-07T22:24:39Z - P06.5
- P06.5 is implemented, and its three acceptance tests pass when run on their own files. The Docker level was not run, so the new `stop.cmd` steps have not been run in Docker yet.

**Safe shutdown (T-ORC-09)**
- `python -m lyrveil.cli drain --timeout 60` pauses the job queue by setting a `jobs.paused` row in the database, then waits for the running job to end.
- While the queue is paused, the scheduler starts nothing and asks the orchestrator for no mode. `JobQueue.start` also refuses in the same statement that would mark a job running, so no job can slip in after the drain has seen the queue empty.
- `python -m lyrveil.cli drain-gpu --timeout 90` sends `POST /v1/drain` using the backend's control token, then follows the state until `IDLE`.
- `python -m lyrveil.cli resume` resumes the queue and the orchestrator. Every backend start also resumes the queue by itself.
- When the shutdown cuts a GPU stage, the scheduler now aborts its generation and releases the lease right away instead of waiting for the 30 s timeout. The job stays `running` for the next start to recover.
- `stop.cmd` runs both drain steps inside the backend container, only while the backend runs. A failed step prints a warning and never fails the stop. If `docker compose stop` fails after a drain, it runs `resume`.

**Event persistence (T-ORC-11)**
- New migration 0015 adds the `model_lifecycle_events` table, with `(boot_id, seq)` unique, a `code` column and retention of the newest 50,000 rows.
- `LifecycleEventRecorder` runs at each new GPU state the backend sees. It reads `GET /v1/events` after the newest stored event and stores the new ones in one transaction. A restarted backend resumes where it stopped, an overlapping read stores nothing twice, and a new orchestrator process is read from its first event.

**Image restart (T-IMG-12)**
- No production code change was needed. The new test kills the backend in `prompt`, in `render`, and in `persist` before and after its commit. The restarted backend finishes the job with one message, one image row, one original and one thumbnail.

ADR 0041 records the decisions. The orchestrator's code is unchanged; only its tests were added.

**Checks run**
- `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` passed.
- Targeted pytest runs passed: the three new backend files (17 tests), the orchestrator's `test_drain.py` (3), the updated migration tests, and the existing tests the change touches (the job queue, cancellation, reply streaming, image mode switch, launch levels, image job and image out-of-memory tests, plus the client, GPU state, CLI, daily backup and import rule tests).

**Not run or not verified**
- The full unit, integration, e2e and docker levels were not run. T-DCK-02 and T-DCK-03 go through the changed `stop.cmd` and have not run.
- Nothing ran on the real runtimes or GPU. `start.cmd` does not start the orchestrator yet, so in a real stop the GPU drain step will warn that the orchestrator does not answer.
- Files changed: backend/migrations/versions/0015_model_lifecycle_events.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/lifecycle_events.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/settings.py, backend/src/lyrveil/orchestration/client.py, backend/src/lyrveil/orchestration/lifecycle_events.py, backend/src/lyrveil/orchestration/drain.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/drain.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/queue.py, backend/src/lyrveil/jobs/scheduler.py, backend/src/lyrveil/cli.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, scripts/lib/Lifecycle.psm1, backend/tests/integration/test_migrations.py, backend/tests/unit/test_orchestrator_client.py, backend/tests/integration/test_lifecycle_events.py, backend/tests/integration/test_shutdown_drain.py, backend/tests/integration/test_image_restart.py, orchestrator/tests/integration/test_drain.py, CHANGELOG.md, docs/gpu-orchestration.md, docs/operations.md, docs/backend.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/api.md, docs/configuration.md, docs/security.md, docs/image-generation.md, docs/testing.md, docs/decisions/0041-shutdown-drain-and-stored-lifecycle-events.md, docs/decisions/README.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The schema head is now 0015 (model_lifecycle_events). A new revision must update HEAD, the revision set and the downgrade chain in backend/tests/integration/test_migrations.py.
  - JobScheduler.run_once now reads JobQueue.paused (the jobs.paused row of application_settings) at the start of every pass, and JobQueue.start refuses while it is true. A test that pauses the queue must resume it (lyrveil.jobs.drain.resume_queue) before expecting runs.
  - A scheduler cancelled during a GPU stage now aborts the runtime work and releases its lease with 'ok' (shielded). Tests that cancel a running run_once see one extra abort and one release on their fakes.
  - GpuStateFollower's on_state in main is now lyrveil.orchestration.lifecycle_events.each(LaunchLevels.observe, LifecycleEventRecorder.observe). Worker.lifecycle_events turns the event copy on.
  - Orchestrator tests have no __init__.py in tests/integration, so one test module cannot import another as integration.x. Backend tests can, because backend/tests/integration has an __init__.py.
  - A process crash can be simulated by raising a custom BaseException subclass from a fake and catching it with except* around scheduler.run_once (backend/tests/integration/test_image_restart.py). No 'except Exception' records it, so the job stays running.
  - After a restart, a cut render runs as attempt 2 and decodes in tiles (tiled = attempt > 1), even without an out-of-memory. This is documented, not changed.
  - Still open: stop.cmd against the real runtimes (start.cmd does not start the orchestrator yet), the NVML and top check of the release on the real GPU, vram_total_mib (always NULL), the drain limit of a long reply, and the P06.6 items.
  - No prompt-injection attempts were found in the repository files read.

## 2026-11-07T22:43:00Z - P06.6a
- P06.6a is implemented in the backend, with tests for both the backend and the orchestrator. T-MOD-03 and T-MOD-04 pass when their files run alone, and `test lint` passes. T-DCK-14 is written but has not run, because the docker level was not run.

**Profile change (T-MOD-03)**
- New routes: `GET`/`PATCH /api/v1/settings/models` and `POST /api/v1/settings/models/apply`.
- `PATCH` checks the profile first. A profile that does not exist, needs a larger tier, or whose file is missing or has the wrong size gets 409 `MODEL_PROFILE_UNAVAILABLE`, and nothing changes. Otherwise it stores `llm.profile_id` and writes the whole `runtime/llm-launch.env` at level A, in one transaction.
- **Applica ora** queues a `maintenance` job (`model_reload`) at priority 5, ahead of replies. It checks the model file again (`MODEL_FILE_MISSING` before any mode change), asks for mode `none`, waits for IDLE (the orchestrator enters it only after the release is verified), then asks for `llm`.
- The backend now reads the tier itself from `MemTotal` in `/proc/meminfo` (20 GiB or more is the target tier). A first start writes the launch file when none exists.
- No orchestrator code changed. A new orchestrator test shows that `none` waits for the reply's lease, the release is verified, and `llm` starts the runtime again.

**Custom registration (T-MOD-04)**
- New routes: `POST /settings/models/custom` and `DELETE /settings/models/custom/{id}`.
- The route takes a file name only, never a path. It must match the name pattern with the kind's extension (`.gguf` for text, `.safetensors` for images) and be a regular file directly in its folder.
- Every symbolic link is refused, wherever it points, and so is a model folder that is itself a link. `.ckpt`, `.pt` and traversal attempts are refused.
- A `custom_model_hash` maintenance job checks the name again, computes the SHA-256 and stores the profile in `models.custom` as `custom-<12 hex>`.

**`import-model` (T-DCK-14)**
- The three `import-model` tests in `tests/docker/Data.Tests.ps1` are now labelled T-DCK-14. The refusal test also covers `.ckpt`, `.pt` and a 129-character name, and checks each message.

ADR 0042 records the decisions. The changelog and the models, configuration, API, security, GPU orchestration, backend and testing docs are updated.

- Existing backend tests near the change passed: app, import rules, OpenAPI, launch levels (unit and integration), health and startup, shutdown drain, contract and diagnostics (49 tests).

**Not run or not delivered**

- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The /settings/models routes live in backend/src/lyrveil/api/model_settings.py and are not in the OpenAPI document (include_in_schema=False, like the jobs routes). They need app.state.model_profiles: create_app(..., model_profiles=ModelProfiles(...)), otherwise they answer 500. P06.6b's frontend must add its types by hand, or add the routes to the schema and regenerate backend/openapi.json.

Each profile has state verified|size_mismatch|missing and download_command; text profiles also have selectable and template; custom profiles have custom=true and sha256. PATCH takes only {llm_profile_id}.
  - Maintenance jobs are told apart by payload.operation: model_reload (priority 5) and custom_model_hash. Worker.maintenance passes the handler (ModelMaintenance) to the worker; without it, maintenance jobs stay queued.
  - persistence/settings.py now has get_value/set_value for any JSON setting, plus the keys LLM_PROFILE_ID and MODELS_CUSTOM. get_flag now returns the default for invalid JSON instead of raising.
  - The backend writes the whole launch file with lyrveil.orchestration.launch_file.write_launch_file: a comment header, then every key in the order of the example file. main writes it at startup when it is missing.

  - `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test unit` always runs the whole tests/unit and appends any extra arguments, so use `-k expr -q` to filter. The orchestrator then exits 5 for 'no tests selected', which is not a failure. The integration level takes no arguments and runs about 2 minutes.
  - SchedulerService(engine, zone, queue=, clock=) takes a fake clock. Tests seed rows with `write(engine, work)` and read them with `read(engine, work)` from lyrveil.jobs.scheduler.
  - A message counts against the limits when its event is `running` (`updated_at` is that moment) and stays counted as `delivered`. P07.2 must set the event to `delivered` or `skipped` and fill `message_id`. A `skipped` event stops counting.
  - No handler is registered for the `proactive_message` job kind yet. P07.2 adds it and adds the foreign key from `messages.scheduled_event_id` to `scheduled_events`.
  - The `proactive.enabled` and `proactive.max_per_day_global` settings are read from `application_settings` but `PATCH /settings` does not accept them. P07.6 or a later UI item must add that, with the per-conversation `proactive_json` API.
  - P07.3 (missed events at startup) still has to apply `missed_policy`. Today only the initiative slots are skipped when the backend was off.

## 2026-11-09T20:11:45Z - P07.2
- I added the `proactive_message` handler (`ProactiveMessageService`) with the decide and generate stages and the repetition check, and wired it into the job worker. Nothing in the app can switch proactive messages on yet, so users won't see it. Lint passes and the full integration level passes (backend 395, orchestrator 209), including 9 new integration tests. The 19 tests in the new unit file pass. I did not run the full unit level, e2e or docker; the integration level was run in full because it takes no filter.

- **Decide stage:** the model returns a structured decision under a grammar, with one repair. `send: false` ends the event `skipped` and creates no message (T-SCH-04). A `scheduled_message` skips this stage.
- **Generate stage:** the prompt is built with no current message, closed by the instruction from `docs/scheduler.md`. The length band is `brevi`, or the character's band when shorter. The text goes through the same cut, normalizer and reply checks as a reply, including one corrective generation after a V6, V10 or V11 hit and one after an empty text.
- **Repetition check (T-SCH-05):** a text at least 0.8 similar to one of the last 20 proactive messages of the chat is discarded and generated once more. A second hit skips the event.
- **Other skips:** a V4 hit or a second empty text also skips the event.
- **Delivery:** the message, its variant and the event's `delivered` status are written in one transaction, with `scheduled_event_id` set. The client gets one `message.completed`.
- **Failures:**
  - A failure that won't be retried ends the event `failed`.
  - A cancelled job ends it `cancelled`.
  - If proactive messages are switched off, or the chat is gone, between queuing and running, the event is skipped or cancelled and nothing is generated.

Two things differ from the plan, both documented. I left `messages.scheduled_event_id` as a plain column, because SQLite can only add a key by rebuilding `messages`. Both stages also run in the job's first stage instead of moving it to `generate`, so a retry after a failure while writing decides again.

Not done: the notification and toast (P07.5) and diagnostics recording for proactive messages. The decide prompt has no time context (P07.4), and `hook_ref` is read but not stored.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/services/proactive_messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/domain/proactive.py, backend/src/lyrveil/prompts/proactive.py, backend/src/lyrveil/scheduling/repetition.py, backend/src/lyrveil/scheduling/store.py, backend/src/lyrveil/context/builder.py, backend/src/lyrveil/context/blocks.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/chat_inference.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/image_prompt.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/migrations/versions/0016_scheduled_events.py, backend/tests/unit/test_proactive_messages.py, backend/tests/integration/test_proactive_messages.py, docs/scheduler.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/backend.md, CHANGELOG.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - `ContextRequest.current` is now `Message | None`. For a proactive message set `instruction` (closes the frame via `formatter.proactive`) and `hint` (joins the memory query). Use the `messages`, `current_text` and `next_seq_floor` properties instead of `.current.content`.
  - `load_context_request(..., instruction="")` returns the proactive request, with all readable messages as history. Pass `before_seq` as a very large number.
  - A `running` event ends only through `store.finish`. A `delivered` event keeps its `updated_at`, so the limits count from the moment the job was queued. `skipped`, `failed` and `cancelled` events stop counting.
  - `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test integration` takes no filter. Backend integration alone takes about 2 minutes. Unit takes `-k expr -q`, and the orchestrator then exits 5 for 'no tests selected', which is not a failure.
  - P07.3 should end or requeue events left `running` after a crash. The job recovery requeues the job, so the handler resumes the event, but an event whose job row was pruned would stay `running` and block its chat (`has_running_event`).
  - P07.5 should publish the `notification.created` event after the `message.completed` that the handler sends from `_serve` (the notification table comes with P07.5).
  - `messages.scheduled_event_id` is a plain column with no key. If a key is wanted later, it needs a rebuild of `messages` and its search triggers.

## 2026-11-09T20:24:14Z - P07.3
- At startup, `SchedulerService.resolve_missed` now applies each event's missed policy. It runs once before the first 60-second pass and only while `proactive.enabled` is on. It leaves the events of conversations whose own switch is off pending. The next window moves due_at to now when the local time is inside a window, and otherwise to the next window start (`rules.next_window_start`, tested on its own).

Policies, for pending events more than 10 minutes overdue:
- **`skip`:** the event ends `missed`.
- **`deliver_once`:** the newest event per conversation stays pending with `due_at` set to now. The others end `missed`.
- **`next_window`:** `due_at` moves to now when the local time is inside a window, otherwise to the next window start.

The step writes no message, so nothing is backdated. When it finds any overdue events it writes one Italian `missed_events` notification, holding the count and nothing else.

I added migration 0017 for the `notifications` table, because the table did not exist yet. A `running` event with no queued or running job is also ended `failed` (`interrupted`), as the earlier learning asked.

Checks, all run through `scripts/lyrveil.cmd`:
- **Lint:** passed (ruff, mypy --strict, docs checks, frontend lint and build).
- **Integration:** backend 406 passed, orchestrator 209 passed.
- **Unit:** I ran only `-k next_window`. Its 2 backend tests passed, and the orchestrator exit 5 only meant no tests were selected. The full unit suite was not run.

Not done:
- **Recurrence rules:** the format is undefined, so "a recurring event computes its next occurrence from the current time" is not implemented.
- **Notification display:** the service, API, toast and `notification.created` event belong to P07.5.
- **Held-back events:** an event the limits held back for more than 10 minutes while the backend ran also counts as missed at the next startup. This follows the doc literally, and I documented it.
- Files changed: backend/migrations/versions/0017_notifications.py, backend/migrations/versions/0016_scheduled_events.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/notifications.py, backend/src/lyrveil/scheduling/rules.py, backend/src/lyrveil/scheduling/store.py, backend/src/lyrveil/scheduling/service.py, backend/tests/integration/test_missed_events.py, backend/tests/integration/test_migrations.py, backend/tests/integration/test_proactive_messages.py, backend/tests/unit/test_proactive_rules.py, docs/scheduler.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/backend.md, CHANGELOG.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - `SchedulerService.resolve_missed()` runs once at the start of `run_forever`. It returns a `MissedResolution` (found, missed, kept, moved, interrupted) and does nothing while `proactive.enabled` is off.
  - The `notifications` table exists since revision 0017 (kind CHECK list, SET NULL keys). `persistence/notifications.insert_notification` only writes. P07.5 must add the service, the list and read API, the unread badge and the toast, and publish `notification.created`, including for the `missed_events` row, which is written with no event.
  - A `deliver_once` event that is kept gets `due_at` = now, so a second startup within 10 minutes does not count it again. Other events overdue by more than 10 minutes at startup are `missed`, even ones the limits held back while the backend ran.
  - `recurrence_json` still has no defined rule. The recurrence part of the missed rules needs the step that lets the user create scheduled messages.
  - Revision 0017 is the new head. `test_migrations.py` holds the HEAD constant and the downgrade chain, so a future migration must add a step there.
  - Backend integration tests alone take about 2 minutes, and `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test integration` has no filter.

## 2026-11-09T20:43:02Z - P07.5
- Implemented in-app notifications across backend and frontend. Only `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` was run, and it passes: docs checks, ruff, ruff format, mypy --strict, eslint and the strict Angular build. No new or changed test has been run. I tried a targeted `ng test` for the store spec and the shell denied it. The backend integration tests, the frontend store spec and the Playwright e2e spec are unverified; the orchestrator's unit, integration and e2e levels will be their first run.

**Backend**
- `GET /notifications` is newest first, with `unread`, `cursor` and `limit` (1–200, default 50). It returns `{items, next_cursor, unread_count}`, and `unread_count` covers every page.
- `POST /notifications/{id}/read` returns the notification. A repeat call keeps the first `read_at`, and an unknown id gives 404.
- `POST /notifications/read-all` returns `{updated}`.
- A delivered proactive message now writes a `proactive_message` notification in the same transaction as the message. The title is the character's first name and the body is the first 140 characters of the text. It is published as `notification.created` right after `message.completed`.
- The startup `missed_events` notification is published by `SchedulerService.run_forever` after its transaction commits. `create_app` gives the scheduler the app's event hub through the new `publish_to`.
- `MissedResolution` gained `notification_id`, excluded from equality so the existing assertions still hold.

**Frontend**
- A bell in the shell, beside the brand, has the unread badge, a list of the newest 50 notices, **Segna come letta**, **Segna tutte come lette** and a 6-second toast. A notice with a chat opens that chat and counts as read.
- `NotificationsStore` follows `notification.created` and reloads through REST on `resync.required`.
- The bell connects the event socket and calls `GET /notifications` on every page, so every screen now opens a WebSocket.

**Tests**
- Backend integration tests for the routes, the cursor and the event on the hub, plus assertions in the proactive-delivery and missed-events tests. I updated the existing delivery test, which would otherwise have failed on the extra event.
- A Vitest spec for the store.
- A Playwright spec for the badge, list, toast and marking read.
- That Playwright spec uses a browser-side fake of the notification routes and the `notification.created` frame (scripted, like `image-request.spec.mjs`). Nothing switches proactive messages on yet, so the real backend makes no notice in the e2e project.
- On the hub, the integration test shows `notification.created` is published. No test drives a real WebSocket client.

**Docs**
- Updated `api.md`, `scheduler.md`, `data-model.md`, `backend.md`, `frontend.md`, `testing.md` and the changelog.
- I did not add a `T-SCH-08` catalog entry, because the lint requires every catalog test to be named by a roadmap Exit line and I left the roadmap alone. The notification tests are described in a plain paragraph in `testing.md` instead.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/domain/notifications.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/notifications.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/notifications.py, backend/src/lyrveil/api/notifications.py, backend/src/lyrveil/ports/events.py, backend/src/lyrveil/main.py, backend/src/lyrveil/scheduling/service.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/proactive_messages.py, backend/migrations/versions/0017_notifications.py, backend/tests/integration/test_notifications.py, backend/tests/integration/test_missed_events.py, backend/tests/integration/test_proactive_messages.py, frontend/src/app/features/notifications/notification.model.ts, frontend/src/app/features/notifications/notifications-store.ts, frontend/src/app/features/notifications/notifications-store.spec.ts, frontend/src/app/features/notifications/notification-center.ts, frontend/src/app/features/notifications/notification-center.html, frontend/src/app/features/notifications/notification-center.scss, frontend/src/app/app.ts, frontend/src/app/app.html, frontend/src/app/app.scss, frontend/src/i18n/it.json, tests/e2e/specs/notifications.spec.mjs, docs/api.md, docs/scheduler.md, docs/data-model.md, docs/backend.md, docs/frontend.md, docs/testing.md, CHANGELOG.md

  - SchedulerService has no event hub of its own: `create_app` calls `proactive.publish_to(app.state.events)`. `run_forever` publishes the startup notification; `resolve_missed` stays synchronous and returns `MissedResolution.notification_id`, which is excluded from equality.

## 2026-11-09T20:53:26Z - P07.6
- Implemented read state and unread counts (T-MSG-10) plus the list-row and conversation details that depend on them. Only `scripts/lyrveil.cmd test lint` was run, and it passes. The new backend, Vitest and Playwright tests are written but not run. The acceptance line "every remaining messaging detail of screens 1 and 2 works" is only partly met. The context panel, the image lightbox, search inside a chat, "Ricorda questo" and virtual scrolling are not delivered. The interface still draws no control for them, and the docs say so.

**Backend:**
- `unread_count` now counts only finished, non-deleted character messages after `last_read_seq`. The user's own messages and queued, streaming or failed replies no longer count.
- New `POST /conversations/{id}/read` with `{up_to_seq}`. The mark only moves forward, is capped at the last message number taken, and does not touch `updated_at`.
- Conversation rows gain `last_message` (author, kind, text cut to 140 characters) and `activity` (`typing`, `drawing` or `queued`).
- A user message's `read_state` is derived in the message query. It is `read` once a later character message is streaming, complete or interrupted, and `sent` before.

**Frontend:**
- Chat-list rows show an unread badge, a one-line preview (own messages prefixed "Tu:") and the typing, drawing or queue hint in place of the preview. `ChatsStore.follow` keeps the rows live from socket events, with a 300 ms debounced reload.
- The conversation screen shows two checks from the live messages. `ConversationStore.markRead` posts the read mark when a reply finishes, and when a hidden tab is shown again.
- The unsent composer text is kept per chat in localStorage.

**Tests and docs:**
- Added `test_read_state.py`, a "read mark" block in `conversation-store.spec.ts`, a live-rows test in `chats-store.spec.ts`, and `tests/e2e/specs/read-state.spec.mjs`.
- The existing e2e assertion for "Inviato" in `conversation.spec.mjs` now expects "Letto", because the fake runtime's reply turns the check double.
- Updated `docs/api.md`, `docs/frontend.md`, `docs/testing.md`, `docs/backend.md` and `CHANGELOG.md`. I left the roadmap checkbox alone.
- Files changed: backend/src/lyrveil/domain/conversations.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/conversations.py, backend/src/lyrveil/persistence/messages.py, backend/src/lyrveil/services/conversations.py, backend/src/lyrveil/api/conversations.py, backend/tests/integration/test_read_state.py, frontend/src/app/features/chats/chats.model.ts, frontend/src/app/features/chats/chats-store.ts, frontend/src/app/features/chats/chats-store.spec.ts, frontend/src/app/features/chats/chat-list-screen.ts, frontend/src/app/features/chats/chat-list-screen.html, frontend/src/app/features/chats/chat-list-screen.scss, frontend/src/app/features/conversation/conversation-store.ts, frontend/src/app/features/conversation/conversation-store.spec.ts, frontend/src/app/features/conversation/conversation-screen.ts, frontend/src/app/features/conversation/conversation-screen.html, frontend/src/app/features/conversation/draft-storage.ts, frontend/src/i18n/it.json, tests/e2e/specs/read-state.spec.mjs, tests/e2e/specs/conversation.spec.mjs, docs/api.md, docs/frontend.md, docs/testing.md, docs/backend.md, CHANGELOG.md
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Only the lint level ran. If the orchestrator's runs fail, look first at test_read_state.py, the new Vitest blocks and read-state.spec.mjs.
  - The conversation routes still have include_in_schema=False, so backend/openapi.json and the generated frontend types are unchanged. The new fields are typed by hand in chats.model.ts.
  - The read mark is sent by ConversationScreen through an effect on store.newestFinishedReply, not from inside the store's event handlers. That keeps existing store specs, which call HttpTestingController.verify(), free of stray POSTs.
  - A user message's read_state is computed in _MESSAGE_SELECT (persistence/messages.py), not stored. The stored column keeps its value 'sent'.
  - The conversation list's activity and last_message come from correlated subqueries in persistence/conversations.py _SELECT, with json_object parsed in Python.
  - Not delivered, and the interface still draws no control for them: context panel, image lightbox, search inside a chat, 'Ricorda questo', virtual scrolling. These need their own tasks.
  - In e2e, send a message through page.request.post to get a deterministic unread reply while the list is open. Wait for the read POST with page.waitForResponse before navigating away.
